Specifically I need to find:
1. Where the scan summary reports things like "security: 1449 findings" and "→ ⚠ 1449 security findings — review before other cleanup"
2. How those counts are computed - do they filter out suppressed/ignored findings?
3. Where "biggest blockers" or blocker guidance is generated
4. How the narrative/coaching system counts findings per detector

Key areas to search:
- `desloppify/intelligence/narrative/` - especially core.py, actions.py, strategy.py, reminders.py, dimensions.py
- `desloppify/app/commands/scan/` - scan reporting
- Any function that counts findings by detector

Be very thorough - look at all the places that count or report finding counts, and check if they filter for `suppressed` status. Also look at the state.py and how findings are stored/queried.

    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }

   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open":
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   135→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   136→        )
   137→    return by_detector
   138→
   139→
   140→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   141→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   142→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   143→    config = {}
   144→    try:
   145→        config = _load_config()
   146→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   147→        config = {}
   148→
   149→    raw_path = default_rel
   150→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   151→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   152→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   153→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   154→
   155→    path = Path(raw_path)
   156→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   157→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   158→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   159→
   160→    try:
   161→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   162→    except ValueError:
   163→        rel_path = str(path)
   164→    return rel_path, path
   165→
   166→
   167→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   168→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   169→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   170→
   171→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   172→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   173→
   174→    in_readme = False
   175→    if generated:
   176→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   177→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   178→            if readme_path.exists():
   179→                try:
   180→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   181→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   182→                    )
   183→                except OSError:
   184→                    in_readme = False
   185→                break
   186→
   187→    recommendation = None
   188→    if generated and not in_readme:
   189→        recommendation = (
   190→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "generated": generated,
   195→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   196→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   197→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   198→    }
   199→
   200→
   201→def _compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   202→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   203→    if not actions:
   204→        return None
   205→    top = actions[0]
   206→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   207→    if not command:
   208→        return None
   209→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   210→    return {
   211→        "command": command,
   212→        "description": description,
   213→    }
   214→
   215→
   216→def _compute_why_now(
   217→    phase: str,
   218→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   219→    primary_action: dict | None,
   220→) -> str:
   221→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""
   222→    hint = str(strategy.get("hint", "")).strip() if isinstance(strategy, dict) else ""
   223→    if hint:
   224→        return hint
   225→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   226→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   227→    phase_default = {
   228→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact findings to establish a clean baseline.",
   229→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   230→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   231→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   232→    }
   233→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open findings first.")
   234→
   235→
   236→def _compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   237→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   238→    return {
   239→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   240→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   241→    }
   242→
   243→
   244→def _compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   245→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   246→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   247→
   248→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   249→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   250→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   251→    if (
   252→        suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD
   253→        or ignored_count >= 100
   254→    ):
   255→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   256→        message = (
   257→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   258→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   259→            else f"{ignored_count} findings hidden by ignore patterns"
   260→        )
   261→        flags.append(
   262→            {
   263→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   264→                "severity": severity,
   265→                "message": message,
   266→            }
   267→        )
   268→
   269→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   270→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   271→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   272→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   273→        flags.append(
   274→            {
   275→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   276→                "severity": severity,
   277→                "message": (
   278→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   279→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix findings"
   280→                ),
   281→            }
   282→        )
   283→
   284→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   285→    return flags
   286→
   287→
   288→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   289→class NarrativeContext:
   290→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""
   291→
   292→    diff: dict | None = None
   293→    lang: str | None = None
   294→    command: str | None = None
   295→    config: dict | None = None
   296→
   297→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   298→    if not lang:
   299→        return raw_history
   300→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   301→
   302→
   303→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   304→    return path_scoped_findings(
   305→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   306→    )
   307→
   308→
   309→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   310→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   311→
   312→
   313→def compute_narrative(
   314→    state: StateModel,
   315→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   316→) -> NarrativeResult:
   317→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   318→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   319→
   320→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   321→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   322→    command = resolved_context.command
   323→    config = resolved_context.config
   324→
   325→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   326→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   327→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   328→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   329→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   330→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   331→
   332→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   333→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   334→
   335→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   336→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   337→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   338→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   339→    action_context = ActionContext(
   340→        by_detector=by_detector,
   341→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   342→        state=state,
   343→        debt=debt,
   344→        lang=lang,
   345→    )
   346→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   347→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   348→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   349→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   350→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   351→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   352→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   353→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   354→    headline = _compute_headline(
   355→        phase,
   356→        dimensions,
   357→        debt,
   358→        milestone,
   359→        diff,
   360→        strict_score,
   361→        overall_score,
   362→        stats,
   363→        history,
   364→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   365→    )
   366→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   367→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    return {
   371→        "phase": phase,
   372→        "headline": headline,
   373→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   374→        "actions": actions,
   375→        "strategy": strategy,
   376→        "tools": tools,
   377→        "debt": debt,
   378→        "milestone": milestone,
   379→        "primary_action": primary_action,
   380→        "why_now": why_now,
   381→        "verification_step": verification_step,
   382→        "risk_flags": risk_flags,
   383→        "strict_target": strict_target,
   384→        "reminders": reminders,
   385→        "reminder_history": updated_reminder_history,
   386→    }
   387→

1→"""Execution strategy engine for narrative lanes and parallelization hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
     9→    _DETECTOR_CASCADE,
    10→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    11→)
    12→
    13→
    14→def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    15→    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    16→    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    17→    for finding in findings.values():
    18→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    19→            continue
    20→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    21→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    22→            detector = "structural"
    23→        file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    24→        if not file_path:
    25→            by_detector.setdefault(detector, set())
    26→            continue
    27→        by_detector.setdefault(detector, set()).add(file_path)
    28→    return by_detector
    29→
    30→
    31→def compute_fixer_leverage(
    32→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    33→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    34→    phase: str,
    35→    _lang: str | None,
    36→) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    37→    """Estimate how much value automated fixers would deliver."""
    38→    auto_fixable = sum(
    39→        action.get("count", 0) for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    40→    )
    41→    total = sum(by_detector.values())
    42→    coverage = auto_fixable / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    43→    total_impact = sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in actions)
    44→    auto_impact = sum(
    45→        action.get("impact", 0)
    46→        for action in actions
    47→        if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    48→    )
    49→    impact_ratio = auto_impact / total_impact if total_impact > 0 else 0.0
    50→
    51→    if coverage == 0:
    52→        recommendation = "none"
    53→    elif coverage > 0.4 or impact_ratio > 0.3:
    54→        recommendation = "strong"
    55→    elif phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression") and coverage > 0.15:
    56→        recommendation = "strong"
    57→    elif coverage > 0.1:
    58→        recommendation = "moderate"
    59→    else:
    60→        recommendation = "none"
    61→
    62→    return {
    63→        "auto_fixable_count": auto_fixable,
    64→        "total_count": total,
    65→        "coverage": round(coverage, 3),
    66→        "impact_ratio": round(impact_ratio, 3),
    67→        "recommendation": recommendation,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _cleanup_lane_actions(actions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    72→    cascade_rank = {detector: idx for idx, detector in enumerate(_DETECTOR_CASCADE)}
    73→
    74→    def sort_key(action: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, float]:
    75→        detector = action.get("detector", "")
    76→        return (cascade_rank.get(detector, 99), -action.get("impact", 0))
    77→
    78→    return sorted(actions, key=sort_key)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _files_for_actions(
    82→    actions: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]]
    83→) -> set[str]:
    84→    files: set[str] = set()
    85→    for action in actions:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector")
    87→        if detector and detector in files_by_detector:
    88→            files |= files_by_detector[detector]
    89→    return files
    90→
    91→
    92→def _group_by_file_overlap(
    93→    action_files: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]],
    94→) -> list[list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]]]:
    95→    """Group actions whose file sets overlap using union-find."""
    96→    item_count = len(action_files)
    97→    if item_count == 0:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    parent = list(range(item_count))
   101→
   102→    def find(index: int) -> int:
   103→        while parent[index] != index:
   104→            parent[index] = parent[parent[index]]
   105→            index = parent[index]
   106→        return index
   107→
   108→    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
   109→        left_root, right_root = find(left), find(right)
   110→        if left_root != right_root:
   111→            parent[left_root] = right_root
   112→
   113→    for left in range(item_count):
   114→        for right in range(left + 1, item_count):
   115→            if action_files[left][1] & action_files[right][1]:
   116→                union(left, right)
   117→
   118→    grouped_indices: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
   119→    for index in range(item_count):
   120→        grouped_indices.setdefault(find(index), []).append(index)
   121→
   122→    return [
   123→        [action_files[index] for index in indices]
   124→        for indices in grouped_indices.values()
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def _refactor_lanes(
   129→    refactor_actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   130→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   131→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   132→    """Split refactor work into independent lanes by file overlap."""
   133→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   134→    action_files = [
   135→        (action, files_by_detector.get(action.get("detector"), set()))
   136→        for action in refactor_actions
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    test_coverage_actions = [
   140→        (action, files)
   141→        for action, files in action_files
   142→        if action.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
   143→    ]
   144→    other_actions = [
   145→        (action, files)
   146→        for action, files in action_files
   147→        if action.get("detector") != "test_coverage"
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    groups = _group_by_file_overlap(other_actions)
   151→    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
   152→        lane_name = f"refactor_{index}" if len(groups) > 1 else "refactor"
   153→        lanes[lane_name] = {
   154→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in group],
   155→            "file_count": len(
   156→                _files_for_actions((action for action, _ in group), files_by_detector)
   157→            ),
   158→            "total_impact": round(
   159→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in group), 1
   160→            ),
   161→            "automation": "manual",
   162→            "run_first": False,
   163→        }
   164→
   165→    if test_coverage_actions:
   166→        lanes["test_coverage"] = {
   167→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in test_coverage_actions],
   168→            "file_count": len(
   169→                _files_for_actions(
   170→                    (action for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), files_by_detector
   171→                )
   172→            ),
   173→            "total_impact": round(
   174→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), 1
   175→            ),
   176→            "automation": "manual",
   177→            "run_first": False,
   178→        }
   179→
   180→    return lanes
   181→
   182→
   183→def compute_lanes(
   184→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   185→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   186→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
   188→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→
   190→    cleanup_actions = _cleanup_lane_actions(
   191→        [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
   192→    )
   193→    reorganize_actions = [
   194→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "reorganize"
   195→    ]
   196→    debt_actions = [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "debt_review"]
   197→    refactor_actions = [
   198→        action
   199→        for action in actions
   200→        if action.get("type") not in {"auto_fix", "reorganize", "debt_review"}
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    if cleanup_actions:
   204→        cleanup_files = _files_for_actions(cleanup_actions, files_by_detector)
   205→        lanes["cleanup"] = {
   206→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in cleanup_actions],
   207→            "file_count": len(cleanup_files),
   208→            "total_impact": round(
   209→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in cleanup_actions), 1
   210→            ),
   211→            "automation": "full",
   212→            "run_first": False,
   213→        }
   214→
   215→    if reorganize_actions:
   216→        lanes["restructure"] = {
   217→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in reorganize_actions],
   218→            "file_count": len(
   219→                _files_for_actions(reorganize_actions, files_by_detector)
   220→            ),
   221→            "total_impact": round(
   222→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in reorganize_actions), 1
   223→            ),
   224→            "automation": "manual",
   225→            "run_first": False,
   226→        }
   227→
   228→    if refactor_actions:
   229→        lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
   230→
   231→    if debt_actions:
   232→        lanes["debt_review"] = {
   233→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in debt_actions],
   234→            "file_count": 0,
   235→            "total_impact": 0.0,
   236→            "automation": "manual",
   237→            "run_first": False,
   238→        }
   239→
   240→    if "cleanup" in lanes:
   241→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   242→            if lane_name == "cleanup":
   243→                continue
   244→            lane_files = _files_for_actions(
   245→                (action for action in actions if action["priority"] in lane["actions"]),
   246→                files_by_detector,
   247→            )
   248→            if cleanup_files & lane_files:
   249→                lanes["cleanup"]["run_first"] = True
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    return lanes
   253→
   254→
   255→def _significant_lane(lane_name: str, lane: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   256→    if lane_name == "debt_review" or lane.get("run_first"):
   257→        return False
   258→    return lane.get("file_count", 0) >= 5 or lane.get("total_impact", 0) >= 1.0
   259→
   260→
   261→def compute_strategy_hint(
   262→    fixer_leverage: dict[str, Any],
   263→    lanes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   264→    can_parallelize: bool,
   265→    phase: str,
   266→) -> str:
   267→    """Generate one- or two-sentence execution strategy guidance."""
   268→    recommendation = fixer_leverage.get("recommendation", "none")
   269→    coverage_pct = round(fixer_leverage.get("coverage", 0) * 100)
   270→    lane_count = sum(
   271→        1
   272→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   273→        if lane_name != "debt_review" and not lane.get("run_first")
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    if recommendation == "strong" and can_parallelize:
   277→        return (
   278→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   279→            f"Then {lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   280→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   281→        )
   282→    if recommendation == "strong":
   283→        return (
   284→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   285→            "Then rescan to verify."
   286→        )
   287→    if can_parallelize:
   288→        return (
   289→            f"{lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   290→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   291→        )
   292→    if phase == "maintenance":
   293→        return "Maintenance mode — address new findings as they appear."
   294→    if phase == "stagnation":
   295→        return "Try a different dimension to break the plateau."
   296→    return "Work through actions in priority order. Rescan after each fix to track progress."
   297→
   298→
   299→def compute_strategy(
   300→    findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   301→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   302→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   303→    phase: str,
   304→    lang: str | None,
   305→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   306→    """Orchestrate strategy computation and annotate actions with lanes."""
   307→    files_by_detector = open_files_by_detector(findings)
   308→    fixer_leverage = compute_fixer_leverage(by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   309→    lanes = compute_lanes(actions, files_by_detector)
   310→
   311→    action_lane: dict[int, str] = {}
   312→    for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   313→        for priority in lane["actions"]:
   314→            action_lane[priority] = lane_name
   315→    for action in actions:
   316→        action["lane"] = action_lane.get(action["priority"])
   317→
   318→    significant_non_blocked = [
   319→        (lane_name, lane)
   320→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   321→        if _significant_lane(lane_name, lane)
   322→    ]
   323→    can_parallelize = len(significant_non_blocked) >= 2
   324→
   325→    hint = compute_strategy_hint(fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, phase)
   326→    review_action = next(
   327→        (action for action in actions if action.get("detector") == "review"), None
   328→    )
   329→    if review_action:
   330→        hint += f" Review: {review_action['count']} finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
   331→
   332→    return {
   333→        "fixer_leverage": fixer_leverage,
   334→        "lanes": {name: {**lane} for name, lane in lanes.items()},
   335→        "can_parallelize": can_parallelize,
   336→        "hint": hint,
   337→    }
   338→

1→"""Headline computation for terminal display."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import _history_strict
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_headline(
     9→    phase: str,
    10→    dimensions: dict,
    11→    debt: dict,
    12→    milestone: str | None,
    13→    diff: dict | None,
    14→    obj_strict: float | None,
    15→    obj_score: float | None,
    16→    stats: dict,
    17→    history: list[dict],
    18→    open_by_detector: dict | None = None,
    19→) -> str | None:
    20→    """Compute one computed sentence for terminal display."""
    21→    # Security callout prefix — prepended to any headline when security findings exist
    22→    security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
    23→    security_prefix = ""
    24→    if security_count > 0:
    25→        s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    26→        security_prefix = f"\u26a0 {security_count} security finding{s} — review before other cleanup. "
    27→
    28→    # Review findings callout — all phases
    29→    review_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review", 0)
    30→    review_suffix = ""
    31→    if review_count > 0:
    32→        s = "s" if review_count != 1 else ""
    33→        uninvestigated = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review_uninvestigated", 0)
    34→        if uninvestigated > 0:
    35→            review_suffix = (
    36→                f" ({review_count} review finding{s} \u2014 run `desloppify show review --status open`)"
    37→            )
    38→        else:
    39→            review_suffix = f" ({review_count} review finding{s} pending)"
    40→
    41→    headline = _compute_headline_inner(
    42→        phase, dimensions, debt, milestone, diff, obj_strict, obj_score, stats, history
    43→    )
    44→
    45→    if headline is None and not security_prefix and not review_suffix:
    46→        return None
    47→    parts = security_prefix + (headline or "") + review_suffix
    48→    return parts or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _compute_headline_inner(
    52→    phase: str,
    53→    dimensions: dict,
    54→    debt: dict,
    55→    milestone: str | None,
    56→    diff: dict | None,
    57→    strict_score: float | None,
    58→    overall_score: float | None,
    59→    stats: dict,
    60→    history: list[dict],
    61→) -> str | None:
    62→    """Compute the base headline (without security prefix)."""
    63→    # Milestone takes priority
    64→    if milestone:
    65→        return milestone
    66→
    67→    # First scan framing
    68→    if phase == "first_scan":
    69→        dims = len(dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])) if dimensions else 0
    70→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    71→        if dims:
    72→            return f"First scan complete. {open_count} open findings across {dims} dimensions."
    73→        return f"First scan complete. {open_count} findings detected."
    74→
    75→    # Regression — acknowledge that drops after fixes are normal
    76→    if phase == "regression":
    77→        prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    78→        curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    79→        if prev is not None and curr is not None:
    80→            drop = round(prev - curr, 1)
    81→            return (
    82→                f"Score shifted {drop} pts — this is normal after structural changes. "
    83→                f"Rescan after your next fix to see the real trend."
    84→            )
    85→
    86→    # Stagnation — suggest which dimension to focus on
    87→    if phase == "stagnation":
    88→        if strict_score is not None:
    89→            stuck_scans = min(len(history), 5)
    90→            wontfix = debt.get("wontfix_count", 0)
    91→            # Point to the specific dimension dragging things down
    92→            lowest_dims = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
    93→            if lowest_dims:
    94→                dim = lowest_dims[0]
    95→                if wontfix > 0:
    96→                    return (
    97→                        f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
    98→                        f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is. "
    99→                        f"{wontfix} wontfix items may also be worth revisiting."
   100→                    )
   101→                return (
   102→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   103→                    f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is."
   104→                )
   105→            if wontfix > 0:
   106→                return (
   107→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   108→                    f"{wontfix} wontfix items — revisit?"
   109→                )
   110→            return (
   111→                f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   112→                f"Try tackling a different dimension."
   113→            )
   114→
   115→    # Leverage point (lowest dimension with biggest impact)
   116→    lowest = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
   117→    if lowest and lowest[0].get("impact", 0) > 0:
   118→        top = lowest[0]
   119→        return (
   120→            f"{top['name']} is your biggest lever: "
   121→            f"{top['issues']} items → +{top['impact']} pts"
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    # Gap callout
   125→    if debt.get("overall_gap", 0) > 5.0:
   126→        gap = debt["overall_gap"]
   127→        worst = debt.get("worst_dimension", "")
   128→        if strict_score is not None and overall_score is not None:
   129→            return (
   130→                f"Strict {strict_score:.1f} vs overall {overall_score:.1f} — "
   131→                f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt, mostly in {worst}"
   132→            )
   133→
   134→    # Maintenance phase
   135→    if phase == "maintenance":
   136→        return (
   137→            f"Strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 — maintenance mode. Watch for regressions."
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    # Middle grind fallback — point toward next item
   141→    if phase == "middle_grind":
   142→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   143→        if lowest:
   144→            top = lowest[0]
   145→            return (
   146→                f"{open_count} findings open. {top['name']} ({top['strict']}%) "
   147→                f"needs attention — run `desloppify next` to start."
   148→            )
   149→        if open_count > 0:
   150→            return f"{open_count} findings open. Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item."
   151→
   152→    # Early momentum fallback — celebrate trajectory
   153→    if phase == "early_momentum" and strict_score is not None:
   154→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   155→        return f"Score {strict_score:.1f}/100 with {open_count} findings open. Keep the momentum going."
   156→
   157→    return None
   158→

1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    38→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    39→    diff_parts = []
    40→    if diff["new"]:
    41→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    42→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    43→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    44→    if diff["reopened"]:
    45→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    46→    if diff_parts:
    47→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    50→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    54→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    55→                "yellow",
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→
    59→
    60→def show_score_delta(
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    prev_overall: float | None,
    63→    prev_objective: float | None,
    64→    prev_strict: float | None,
    65→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    66→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    67→):
    68→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    69→    stats = state["stats"]
    70→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    71→
    72→    if (
    73→        new.overall is None
    74→        or new.objective is None
    75→        or new.strict is None
    76→        or new.verified is None
    77→    ):
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    81→                "yellow",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    87→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = _format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    88→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    89→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = _format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    90→    print(
    91→        "  Scores: "
    92→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
    93→        + colorize(
    94→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
    95→            objective_color,
    96→        )
    97→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
    98→        + colorize(
    99→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   100→            verified_color,
   101→        )
   102→    )
   103→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   104→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   105→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   106→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   107→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   108→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   112→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   113→                "yellow",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→
   117→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   118→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   119→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   120→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   121→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   122→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   123→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   124→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   125→
   126→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   127→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   128→        status = integrity.get("status")
   129→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   130→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   131→        if status == "penalized":
   132→            print(
   133→                colorize(
   134→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   135→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   136→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   137→                    "red",
   138→                )
   139→            )
   140→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   141→            if streak >= 2:
   142→                print(
   143→                    colorize(
   144→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   145→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   146→                        "yellow",
   147→                    )
   148→                )
   149→        elif status == "warn":
   150→            print(
   151→                colorize(
   152→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   153→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   154→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   155→                    "yellow",
   156→                )
   157→            )
   158→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   159→            if streak >= 2:
   160→                print(
   161→                    colorize(
   162→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   163→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   164→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   165→                        "yellow",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→
   169→
   170→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   171→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   172→    try:
   173→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   174→
   175→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   176→        if concerns:
   177→            print(
   178→                colorize(
   179→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   180→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   181→                    "cyan",
   182→                )
   183→            )
   184→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   185→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   186→
   187→
   188→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   189→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   190→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   191→    for message, style in lines:
   192→        print(colorize(message, style))
   193→    return target, gap
   194→
   195→
   196→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   197→

1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    85→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    86→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    87→    )
    88→
    89→
    90→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    91→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    92→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    93→    supported: set[str] | None,
    94→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    95→    state: StateModel,
    96→) -> None:
    97→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    98→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    99→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   100→            continue
   101→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   102→        if count == 0:
   103→            continue
   104→
   105→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   106→        available_fixers = [
   107→            fixer
   108→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   109→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   110→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   111→        ]
   112→        if not available_fixers:
   113→            actions.append(
   114→                {
   115→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   116→                    "detector": detector,
   117→                    "count": count,
   118→                    "description": (
   119→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   120→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   121→                    ),
   122→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   123→                    "impact": impact,
   124→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   125→                }
   126→            )
   127→            continue
   128→
   129→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   130→        actions.append(
   131→            {
   132→                "type": "auto_fix",
   133→                "detector": detector,
   134→                "count": count,
   135→                "description": (
   136→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   137→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   138→                ),
   139→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   140→                "impact": impact,
   141→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   142→            }
   143→        )
   144→
   145→
   146→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   147→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   148→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   149→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
   152→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   153→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
   154→            continue
   155→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   156→        if count == 0:
   157→            continue
   158→
   159→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   160→        actions.append(
   161→            {
   162→                "type": "reorganize",
   163→                "detector": detector,
   164→                "count": count,
   165→                "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}",
   166→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   167→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   168→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _build_refactor_entry(
   174→    detector: str,
   175→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   176→    count: int,
   177→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   178→) -> ActionItem:
   179→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   180→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   181→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   182→
   183→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   184→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   185→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   186→    elif detector == "review":
   187→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   188→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   189→        description = (
   190→            f"{count} review finding{suffix} need investigation — "
   191→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   192→        )
   193→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   194→    else:
   195→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   196→        description = f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}"
   197→
   198→    return {
   199→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   200→        "detector": detector,
   201→        "count": count,
   202→        "description": description,
   203→        "command": command,
   204→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   205→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   206→    }
   207→
   208→
   209→def _append_refactor_actions(
   210→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   211→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   212→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   213→) -> None:
   214→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   215→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   216→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   217→            continue
   218→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   219→        if count == 0:
   220→            continue
   221→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   222→
   223→
   224→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   225→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   226→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   227→    if gap <= 2.0:
   228→        return
   229→    actions.append(
   230→        {
   231→            "type": "debt_review",
   232→            "detector": None,
   233→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   234→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   235→            "gap": gap,
   236→        }
   237→    )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   241→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   242→    type_order = {
   243→        "issue_queue": 0,
   244→        "auto_fix": 1,
   245→        "reorganize": 2,
   246→        "refactor": 3,
   247→        "manual_fix": 4,
   248→        "debt_review": 5,
   249→    }
   250→    actions.sort(
   251→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   252→    )
   253→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   254→        action["priority"] = index
   255→    return actions
   256→
   257→
   258→def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
   259→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
   260→    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
   261→    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
   262→    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)
   263→
   264→    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
   265→    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   266→    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   267→    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
   268→
   269→    return _assign_priorities(actions)
   270→
```

1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""

     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
    11→    _FEEDBACK_URL,

    20→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for finding in findings.values():
    24→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if finding.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
    44→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    45→    ]
    46→    if not auto_fix_actions:
    47→        return []
    48→    total = sum(action.get("count", 0) for action in auto_fix_actions)
    49→    if total <= 0:
    50→        return []
    51→    first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run")
    52→    return [
    53→        {
    54→            "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    55→            "message": f"{total} findings are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    56→            "command": first_cmd,
    57→        }
    58→    ]
    59→
    60→
    61→def _rescan_needed_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    62→    if command not in {"fix", "resolve", "ignore"}:
    63→        return []
    64→    return [
    65→        {
    66→            "type": "rescan_needed",
    67→            "message": "Rescan to verify — cascading effects may create new findings.",
    68→            "command": "desloppify scan",
    69→        }
    70→    ]
    71→
    72→
    73→def _badge_reminder(strict_score: float | None, badge: dict) -> list[dict]:
    74→    eligible_for_badge = (
    75→        strict_score is not None
    76→        and strict_score >= 90
    77→        and badge.get("generated")
    78→        and not badge.get("in_readme")
    79→    )
    80→    if not eligible_for_badge:
    81→        return []
    82→    badge_path = str(badge.get("path") or "scorecard.png")
    83→    return [
    84→        {
    85→            "type": "badge_recommendation",
    86→            "message": (
    87→                "Score is above 90! Add the scorecard to your README: "
    88→                f'<img src="{badge_path}" width="100%">'
    89→            ),
    90→            "command": None,
    91→        }
    92→    ]
    93→
    94→
    95→def _wontfix_debt_reminders(state: StateModel, debt: dict, command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    96→    reminders: list[dict] = []
    97→    if debt.get("trend") == "growing":
    98→        reminders.append(
    99→            {
   100→                "type": "wontfix_growing",
   101→                "message": "Wontfix debt is growing. Review stale decisions: `desloppify show --status wontfix`.",
   102→                "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   103→            }
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   107→    if scan_count < 20 or command != "scan":
   108→        return reminders
   109→
   110→    stale_wontfix = []
   111→    for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values():
   112→        if finding.get("status") != "wontfix":
   113→            continue
   114→        resolved_at = finding.get("resolved_at")
   115→        if not resolved_at:
   116→            continue
   117→        try:
   118→            age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - _dt.fromisoformat(resolved_at)).days
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   120→            log_best_effort_failure(
   121→                logger, f"parse wontfix timestamp {resolved_at!r}", exc
   122→            )
   123→            continue
   124→        if age_days > 60:
   125→            stale_wontfix.append(finding)
   126→
   127→    if stale_wontfix:
   128→        reminders.append(
   129→            {
   130→                "type": "wontfix_stale",
   131→                "message": (
   132→                    f"{len(stale_wontfix)} wontfix item(s) are >60 days old. "
   133→                    f"Has anything changed? Review with: "
   134→                    f'`desloppify show "*" --status wontfix`'
   135→                ),
   136→                "command": 'desloppify show "*" --status wontfix',
   137→            }
   138→        )
   139→    return reminders
   140→
   141→
   142→def _ignore_suppression_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   143→    """Nudge when ignores/suppression are high enough to mask signal quality."""
   144→    integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) or {}
   145→    ignored = int(integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→    suppressed_pct = float(integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if ignored < 10 and suppressed_pct < 30.0:
   148→        return []
   149→    return [
   150→        {
   151→            "type": "ignore_suppression_high",
   152→            "message": (
   153→                f"Ignore suppression is high ({ignored} ignored, {suppressed_pct:.1f}% suppressed). "
   154→                "Revisit broad ignore patterns and resolve stale suppressions."
   155→            ),
   156→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   157→        }
   158→    ]
   159→
   160→
   161→def _stagnation_reminders(dimensions: dict) -> list[dict]:
   162→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   163→    for dim in dimensions.get("stagnant_dimensions", []):
   164→        strict = dim.get("strict", 0)
   165→        if strict >= 99:
   166→            message = (
   167→                f"{dim['name']} has been at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   168→                f"The remaining items may be worth marking as wontfix if they're intentional."
   169→            )
   170→        else:
   171→            message = (
   172→                f"{dim['name']} has been stuck at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   173→                f"Try tackling it from a different angle — run `desloppify next` to find the right entry point."
   174→            )
   175→        reminders.append(
   176→            {
   177→                "type": "stagnant_nudge",
   178→                "message": message,
   179→                "command": None,
   180→            }
   181→        )
   182→    return reminders
   183→
   184→
   185→def _dry_run_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   186→    if not actions or actions[0].get("type") != "auto_fix":
   187→        return []
   188→    return [
   189→        {
   190→            "type": "dry_run_first",
   191→            "message": "Always --dry-run first, review changes, then apply.",
   192→            "command": None,
   193→        }
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→
   197→def _zone_classification_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   198→    zone_dist = state.get("zone_distribution")
   199→    if not zone_dist:
   200→        return []
   201→    non_prod = sum(value for key, value in zone_dist.items() if key != "production")
   202→    if non_prod <= 0:
   203→        return []
   204→    total = sum(zone_dist.values())
   205→    parts = [
   206→        f"{value} {key}"
   207→        for key, value in sorted(zone_dist.items())
   208→        if key != "production" and value > 0
   209→    ]
   210→    return [
   211→        {
   212→            "type": "zone_classification",
   213→            "message": (
   214→                f"{non_prod} of {total} files classified as non-production "
   215→                f"({', '.join(parts)}). "
   216→                f"Override with `desloppify zone set <file> production` "
   217→                f"if any are misclassified."
   218→            ),
   219→            "command": "desloppify zone show",
   220→        }
   221→    ]
   222→
   223→
   224→def _fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float]) -> list[dict]:
   225→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   226→    for (detector, zone), rate in fp_rates.items():
   227→        if rate <= 0.3:
   228→            continue
   229→        pct = round(rate * 100)
   230→        reminders.append(
   231→            {
   232→                "type": f"fp_calibration_{detector}_{zone}",
   233→                "message": (
   234→                    f"{pct}% of {detector} findings in {zone} zone are false positives. "
   235→                    f"Consider reviewing detection rules for {zone} files."
   236→                ),
   237→                "command": None,
   238→            }
   239→        )
   240→    return reminders
   241→
   242→
   243→def _review_queue_reminders(
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→    scoped_findings: dict,
   246→    command: str | None,
   247→    strict_score: float | None,
   248→) -> list[dict]:
   249→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   250→    open_review = [
   251→        finding
   252→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   253→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   254→    ]
   255→    if open_review:
   256→        uninvestigated = [
   257→            finding
   258→            for finding in open_review
   259→            if not finding.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   260→        ]
   261→        if uninvestigated:
   262→            reminders.append(
   263→                {
   264→                    "type": "review_findings_pending",
   265→                    "message": f"{len(uninvestigated)} review finding(s) need investigation. "
   266→                    f"Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue.",
   267→                    "command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   268→                }
   269→            )
   270→
   271→    if command == "resolve" and state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   272→        reminders.append(
   273→            {
   274→                "type": "rereview_needed",
   275→                "message": "Subjective results may be stale after resolve. Re-run "
   276→                "`desloppify review --prepare` to refresh, or reset with "
   277→                "`desloppify scan --reset-subjective` before a clean rerun.",
   278→                "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   283→    if not review_cache.get("files"):
   284→        current_strict = strict_score or 0
   285→        if current_strict >= 80:
   286→            reminders.append(
   287→                {
   288→                    "type": "review_not_run",
   289→                    "message": (
   290→                        "Mechanical checks look good! Run a subjective design review "
   291→                        "to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare"
   292→                    ),
   293→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   294→                }
   295→            )
   296→
   297→    return reminders
   298→
   299→
   300→def _has_open_findings(state: StateModel) -> bool:
   301→    """True when any non-suppressed open findings remain in the queue."""
   302→    return any(
   303→        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   304→        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   305→    )
   306→
   307→
   308→def _stale_assessment_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   309→    """Nudge when mechanical changes have staled subjective assessments."""
   310→    if _has_open_findings(state):
   311→        return []
   312→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   313→    stale_dims = [
   314→        dim_key
   315→        for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items()
   316→        if isinstance(assessment, dict) and assessment.get("needs_review_refresh")
   317→    ]
   318→    if not stale_dims:
   319→        return []
   320→    dims_arg = ",".join(stale_dims[:10])
   321→    return [
   322→        {
   323→            "type": "stale_assessments",
   324→            "message": (
   325→                f"{len(stale_dims)} subjective dimension{'s' if len(stale_dims) != 1 else ''} "
   326→                f"stale after mechanical changes — re-review with: "
   327→                f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`"
   328→            ),
   329→            "command": f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}",
   330→        }
   331→    ]
   332→
   333→
   334→def _review_staleness_reminder(state: StateModel, config: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   335→    review_max_age = (config or {}).get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   336→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   337→    if review_max_age <= 0 or not review_cache.get("files"):
   338→        return []
   339→    try:
   340→        oldest_str = min(
   341→            review["reviewed_at"]
   342→            for review in review_cache["files"].values()
   343→            if review.get("reviewed_at")
   344→        )
   345→        oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   346→        age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - oldest).days
   347→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   348→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "parse oldest review timestamp", exc)
   349→        return []
   350→    if age_days <= review_max_age:
   351→        return []
   352→    return [
   353→        {
   354→            "type": "review_stale",
   355→            "message": f"Design review is {age_days} days old — run: desloppify review --prepare",
   356→            "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   357→        }
   358→    ]
   359→
   360→
   361→def _feedback_reminder(
   362→    state: StateModel,
   363→    phase: str,
   364→    command: str | None,
   365→    fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float],
   366→) -> list[dict]:
   367→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   368→    if scan_count < 2 or command != "scan":
   369→        return []
   370→    high_fp_dets = [
   371→        detector for (detector, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3
   372→    ]
   373→    if high_fp_dets:
   374→        nudge_msg = (
   375→            f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   376→            f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at "
   377→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} with the file and expected behavior — "
   378→            f"it helps calibrate detection for everyone."
   379→        )
   380→    elif phase == "stagnation":
   381→        nudge_msg = (
   382→            f"Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   383→            f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at "
   384→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} describing what you expected. "
   385→            f"Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation."
   386→        )
   387→    else:
   388→        nudge_msg = (
   389→            f"If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   390→            f"or findings that seem off, file an issue at {_FEEDBACK_URL} — "
   391→            f"include the file and what you expected. It helps improve the tool."
   392→        )
   393→    return [
   394→        {
   395→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   396→            "message": nudge_msg,
   397→            "command": None,
   398→        }
   399→    ]
   400→
   401→
   402→_REMINDER_METADATA: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {
   403→    # Highest urgency operational nudges.
   404→    "rescan_needed": (1, "high"),
   405→    "ignore_suppression_high": (1, "high"),
   406→    "review_findings_pending": (1, "high"),
   407→    "rereview_needed": (1, "high"),
   408→    # Medium urgency quality/process nudges.
   409→    "wontfix_growing": (2, "medium"),
   410→    "wontfix_stale": (2, "medium"),
   411→    "stagnant_nudge": (2, "medium"),
   412→    "review_stale": (2, "medium"),
   413→    "stale_assessments": (2, "medium"),
   414→    "auto_fixers_available": (2, "medium"),
   415→    "zone_classification": (2, "medium"),
   416→    "fp_calibration": (2, "medium"),
   417→    # Informational nudges.
   418→    "badge_recommendation": (3, "low"),
   419→    "dry_run_first": (3, "low"),
   420→    "feedback_nudge": (3, "low"),
   421→    "review_not_run": (3, "low"),
   422→    "report_scores": (3, "low"),
   423→}
   424→
   425→
   426→def _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   427→    """Attach stable priority/severity metadata and sort by priority."""
   428→    decorated: list[dict] = []
   429→    for reminder in reminders:
   430→        reminder_type = str(reminder.get("type", ""))
   431→        key = reminder_type
   432→        if reminder_type.startswith("fp_calibration_"):
   433→            key = "fp_calibration"
   434→        priority, severity = _REMINDER_METADATA.get(key, (3, "low"))
   435→        clone = dict(reminder)
   436→        clone.setdefault("priority", priority)
   437→        clone.setdefault("severity", severity)
   438→        decorated.append(clone)
   439→    decorated.sort(key=lambda item: (int(item.get("priority", 3)), str(item.get("type", ""))))
   440→    return decorated
   441→
   442→
   443→def _report_scores_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   444→    if command != "scan":
   445→        return []
   446→    return [
   447→        {
   448→            "type": "report_scores",
   449→            "message": (
   450→                "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
   451→                "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
   452→                "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
   453→            ),
   454→            "command": None,
   455→            "no_decay": True,
   456→        }
   457→    ]
   458→
   459→
   460→def _apply_decay(
   461→    reminders: list[dict], reminder_history: dict
   462→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   463→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   464→    for reminder in reminders:
   465→        if reminder.get("no_decay"):
   466→            filtered.append(reminder)
   467→            continue
   468→        count = reminder_history.get(reminder["type"], 0)
   469→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   470→            filtered.append(reminder)
   471→
   472→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   473→    for reminder in filtered:
   474→        updated_history[reminder["type"]] = updated_history.get(reminder["type"], 0) + 1
   475→    return filtered, updated_history
   476→
   477→
   478→def _compute_reminders(
   479→    state: StateModel,
   480→    lang: str | None,
   481→    phase: str,
   482→    debt: dict,
   483→    actions: list[dict],
   484→    dimensions: dict,
   485→    badge: dict,
   486→    command: str | None,
   487→    config: dict | None = None,
   488→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   489→    """Compute context-specific reminders, suppressing those shown too many times."""
   490→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific rules.
   491→
   492→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
   493→    reminder_history = state.get("reminder_history", {})
   494→    scoped_findings = path_scoped_findings(
   495→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   496→    )
   497→    fp_rates = _compute_fp_rates(scoped_findings)
   498→
   499→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   500→    reminders.extend(_report_scores_reminder(command))
   501→    reminders.extend(_auto_fixer_reminder(actions))
   502→    reminders.extend(_rescan_needed_reminder(command))
   503→    reminders.extend(_badge_reminder(strict_score, badge))
   504→    reminders.extend(_wontfix_debt_reminders(state, debt, command))
   505→    reminders.extend(_ignore_suppression_reminder(state))
   506→    reminders.extend(_stagnation_reminders(dimensions))
   507→    reminders.extend(_dry_run_reminder(actions))
   508→    reminders.extend(_zone_classification_reminder(state))
   509→    reminders.extend(_fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates))
   510→    reminders.extend(
   511→        _review_queue_reminders(state, scoped_findings, command, strict_score)
   512→    )
   513→    reminders.extend(_stale_assessment_reminder(state))
   514→    reminders.extend(_review_staleness_reminder(state, config))
   515→    reminders.extend(_feedback_reminder(state, phase, command, fp_rates))
   516→
   517→    reminders = _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders)
   518→    return _apply_decay(reminders, reminder_history)
   519→
```
