```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",
    12→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    13→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    14→    "add_ignore",
    15→    "make_finding",
    16→]
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    64→            continue
    65→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    66→            continue
    67→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    68→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    69→            in_scope += 1
    70→        else:
    71→            out_of_scope += 1
    72→
    73→    return {
    74→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    75→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    76→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    81→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    82→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    83→
    84→
    85→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    86→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    87→) -> str | None:
    88→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    89→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    90→        if "*" in pattern:
    91→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    92→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    93→                return pattern
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        if "::" in pattern:
    97→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
    98→                return pattern
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   102→            return pattern
   103→
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   109→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   114→    ]
   115→    now = utc_now()
   116→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   117→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   120→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   121→        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   122→            finding["status"] = "open"
   123→            finding["resolved_at"] = None
   124→            finding["note"] = (
   125→                "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   126→            )
   127→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   132→    return len(matched_ids)
   133→
   134→
   135→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:
   141→        ignores.append(pattern)
   142→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   143→
   144→
   145→def make_finding(
   146→    detector: str,
   147→    file: str,
   148→    name: str,
   149→    *,
   150→    tier: int,
   151→    confidence: str,
   152→    summary: str,
   153→    detail: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> Finding:
   155→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   156→    rfile = rel(file)
   157→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   158→    now = utc_now()
   159→    return {
   160→        "id": finding_id,
   161→        "detector": detector,
   162→        "file": rfile,
   163→        "tier": tier,
   164→        "confidence": confidence,
   165→        "summary": summary,
   166→        "detail": detail or {},
   167→        "status": "open",
   168→        "note": None,
   169→        "first_seen": now,
   170→        "last_seen": now,
   171→        "resolved_at": None,
   172→        "reopen_count": 0,
   173→    }
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→
```

```
    46→    merge_scan_results,
    47→    persist_reminder_history,
    48→    prepare_scan_runtime,
    49→    resolve_noise_snapshot,
    50→    run_scan_generation,
    51→)
    52→from desloppify.core.query import write_query
    53→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    54→
    55→
    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    57→    """Print the scan header line."""
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
```

```
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod

     9→
    10→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    11→    try:
    12→        parsed = float(value)
    13→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    14→        return default
    15→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    16→
    17→
    18→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    19→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    20→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    23→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    24→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    25→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    26→    return {}
    27→
    28→
    29→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    30→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    31→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    32→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    33→        return []
    34→
    35→    warnings: list[str] = []
    36→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    40→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    41→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    42→            continue
    43→
    44→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    45→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    46→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    47→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    48→
    49→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    50→        if impact:
    51→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    52→        if remediation:
    53→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    54→        warnings.append(line)
    55→    return warnings
    56→
    57→
    58→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    59→    diff: dict,
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    lang,
    62→    *,
    63→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    64→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    65→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    66→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    67→    warnings: list[str] = []
    68→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    69→        warnings.append(
    70→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    71→        )
    72→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    73→        warnings.append(
    74→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    75→        )
    76→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    77→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    78→    if chronic_count > 0:
    79→        warnings.append(
    80→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    81→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    82→        )
    83→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
    84→
    85→    for warning in warnings:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
    87→    if warnings:
    88→        print()
    89→
    90→    # Single narrative headline
    91→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    92→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    93→        state,
    94→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
    95→            diff=diff,
    96→            lang=lang_name,
    97→            command="scan",
    98→        ),
    99→    )
   100→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   101→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   102→
   103→    # Two pointers
   104→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.", "dim"))
   105→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.", "dim"))
   106→    print()
   107→
   108→    return warnings, narrative
   109→
   110→
   111→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   112→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   113→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   114→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   115→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   116→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   117→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   118→    confidence_reduced = (
   119→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   120→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   124→        return
   125→
   126→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   127→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   128→    strict_gap = (
   129→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   133→    actionable = (
   134→        stats.get("open", 0)
   135→        + wontfix
   136→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   137→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   138→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   139→    )
   140→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   141→
   142→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   143→
   144→    if wontfix > 5:
   145→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   146→            style = "red"
   147→            msg = (
   148→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   149→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   150→            )
   151→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   152→            style = "yellow"
   153→            msg = (
   154→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   155→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   156→            )
   157→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   158→            style = "yellow"
   159→            msg = (
   160→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   161→                "pts below lenient"
   162→            )
   163→        else:
   164→            style = "dim"
   165→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   166→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   167→
   168→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   169→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   170→        if dim_scores:
   171→            gaps = []
   172→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   173→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   174→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   175→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   176→                if gap > 0:
   177→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   178→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   179→            if gaps:
   180→                top = gaps[:2]
   181→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   182→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   183→
   184→    if ignored > 0:
   185→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   186→        print(
   187→            colorize(
   188→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   189→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   190→                style,
   191→            )
   192→        )
   193→        print(
   194→            colorize(

   196→                "dim",
   197→            )
   198→        )
   199→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:

   203→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   204→                "dim",
   205→            )
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    if confidence_reduced:
   209→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   210→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   211→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(
```

```
111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open":
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   135→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   136→        )
   137→    return by_detector
   138→
```

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me compile the findings into a comprehensive report:

## Summary Report: Security Blocker Guidance Finding Count Issue

I've completed a thorough analysis of the desloppify codebase to understand how security blocker guidance counts findings. Here's what I found:

### **CRITICAL ISSUE IDENTIFIED: Suppressed Findings Are Being Counted**

The problem is in the narrative counting system. Suppressed/ignored findings are **NOT being filtered out** when computing counts for blocker guidance.

---

## Where Finding Counts Are Computed

### 1. **Core Counting Function: `_count_open_by_detector()` (Line 111 of `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/core.py`)**

```python
def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors."""
    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    for f in findings.values():
        if f["status"] != "open":
            continue
        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
            detector = "structural"
        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
        # ... more code
    return by_detector
```

**THE BUG**: This function only checks `if f["status"] != "open"` but **DOES NOT check `f.get("suppressed")`**. This means:
- Findings with `status == "open"` AND `suppressed == True` are counted
- These suppressed findings inflate the reported counts

### 2. **Where the Counts Get Used**

The `by_detector` dict from `_count_open_by_detector()` is passed through the narrative pipeline and used in multiple places:

#### A. **Headline/Security Callout** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/headline.py`, lines 22-26):
```python
security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
security_prefix = ""
if security_count > 0:
    s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    security_prefix = f"⚠ {security_count} security finding{s} — review before other cleanup. "
```

**Result**: Displays `⚠ 1449 security findings — review before other cleanup` even if many are suppressed.

#### B. **Action Engine** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/action_engine.py`, lines 90-143):
```python
def _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, by_detector, ...):
    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
        # ... 
        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
        if count == 0:
            continue
        # Creates action items with inflated counts
        actions.append({
            "type": "auto_fix",
            "count": count,
            "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — run `desloppify fix...`"
        })
```

**Result**: "Biggest blockers" guidance uses these inflated counts to prioritize actions.

#### C. **Strategy Hints** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/strategy_engine.py`, lines 31-68):
```python
def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for finding in findings.values():
        if finding["status"] != "open":
            continue
        # ... no suppressed check here either
```

---

## What's Correct vs. What's Wrong

### ✅ **Correct Filtering** (for comparison):

These functions DO correctly filter out suppressed findings:

1. **`reminders.py` line 301**: `_has_open_findings()`
   ```python
   def _has_open_findings(state: StateModel) -> bool:
       """True when any non-suppressed open findings remain in the queue."""
       return any(
           f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
           for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
       )
   ```

2. **`scan_reporting_dimensions.py` line 138**: Scorecard check
   ```python
   has_open = any(
       f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
       for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   )
   ```

3. **`reminders.py` line 497**: FP rate computation uses scoped findings without suppressed check, but other filters apply elsewhere

### ❌ **Wrong - Missing Suppressed Check**:

1. **`core.py` line 119**: `_count_open_by_detector()`
   ```python
   if f["status"] != "open":
       continue
   # Missing: and not f.get("suppressed")
   ```

2. **`strategy_engine.py` line 18**: `open_files_by_detector()`
   ```python
   if finding["status"] != "open":
       continue
   # Missing: and not f.get("suppressed")
   ```

3. **`strategy_engine.py` line 83**: `_fixer_has_applicable_findings()` for smells detector
   ```python
   if detector != "smells":
       return True
   smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
   return any(
       f.get("status") == "open"
       and f.get("detector") == "smells"
       and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
       for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
   )
   # Missing: and not f.get("suppressed")
   ```

---

## Flow of Data

```
scan.py
  └─> show_post_scan_analysis()
        └─> compute_narrative(state)
              └─> _count_open_by_detector(findings)  [NO SUPPRESSED FILTER]
                    ├─> by_detector dict (INFLATED)
                    └─> Used by:
                        1. _compute_headline()
                           └─> security_prefix = f"⚠ {security_count} findings..."
                        2. compute_actions() with by_detector
                           └─> ActionEngine._append_*_actions()
                               └─> Creates actions with inflated counts
                        3. compute_strategy()
                           └─> open_files_by_detector() [ALSO NO FILTER]
                           └─> Lanes/hints use inflated file counts
```

---

## Specific Examples of Misleading Output

Given `state.json` with:
- 1449 "security" findings where **status == "open"** and **suppressed == True**
- 0 unsuppressed security findings

**Current (WRONG):**
```
⚠ 1449 security findings — review before other cleanup
→ Run auto fixer: 1449 security findings — run `desloppify fix security_fixer --dry-run` to preview
```

**Expected (CORRECT):**
```
⚠ 0 security findings — review before other cleanup
(no security action shown)
```

---

## How Suppressed Findings Are Created

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py`, lines 115-122:

```python
def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern."""
    for finding_id in matched_ids:
        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
        finding["suppressed"] = True
        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
            finding["status"] = "open"  # Re-opens suppressed findings!
```

**Key insight**: Suppressed findings have their status set/kept as `"open"` but are marked with `suppressed: True` to indicate they're hidden. The narrative counting system needs to respect this flag.

---

## Summary of Fixes Needed

1. **`core.py` line 119**: Add `and not f.get("suppressed")` to `_count_open_by_detector()`
2. **`strategy_engine.py` line 18**: Add `and not finding.get("suppressed")` to `open_files_by_detector()`
3. **`strategy_engine.py` line 83**: Add `and not f.get("suppressed")` to `_fixer_has_applicable_findings()`

These changes will ensure that blocker guidance reflects only actionable (non-suppressed) findings, eliminating the misleading "1449 security findings" message when those findings are ignored by patterns.

```
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
   151→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   152→
   153→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   154→    show_score_delta(
   155→        runtime.state,
   156→        merge.prev_overall,
   157→        merge.prev_objective,
   158→        merge.prev_strict,
   159→        merge.prev_verified,
   160→    )
   161→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   162→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   163→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   164→
   165→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   166→
   167→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   168→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   169→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   170→
   171→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   172→        merge.diff,
   173→        runtime.state,
   174→        runtime.lang,
   175→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   176→    )
   177→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   178→
   179→    write_query(
   180→        build_scan_query_payload(
   181→            runtime.state,
   182→            runtime.config,
   183→            runtime.profile,
   184→            merge.diff,
   185→            warnings,
   186→            narrative,
   187→            merge,
   188→            noise,
   189→        ),
   190→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   194→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   195→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   196→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   197→        badge_path = badge_emit
   198→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   199→    auto_update_skill()
   200→
   201→
   202→__all__ = [
   203→    "cmd_scan",
   204→]
```

```
    87→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:

    92→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    93→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
    94→    try:
    95→        parsed = int(value)

    98→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    99→    return parsed, valid

   101→
   102→def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   103→    """Load config from disk, auto-migrating from state files if needed.
   104→
   105→    Fills missing keys with defaults. If no config.json exists, attempts
   106→    migration from state-*.json files.
   107→    """
   108→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   109→    if p.exists():
   110→        try:
   111→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   112→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   113→            config = {}
   114→    else:
   115→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   116→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   117→
   118→    changed = False
   119→
   120→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   121→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   122→        if key not in config:
   123→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   124→            changed = True
   125→        elif key == "badge_path":
   126→            try:
   127→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   128→                if normalized != config[key]:
   129→                    config[key] = normalized
   130→                    changed = True
   131→            except ValueError:
   132→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   133→                changed = True
   134→
   135→    if changed and p.exists():
   136→        try:
   137→            save_config(config, p)
   138→        except OSError as exc:
   139→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   140→
   141→    return config
   142→
   143→
   144→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   145→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   146→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   147→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   148→
   149→
   150→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   151→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   152→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   153→    if pattern not in ignores:
   154→        ignores.append(pattern)
   155→
   156→
   157→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   158→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   159→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   161→        meta = {}
   162→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   163→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   164→
   165→
   166→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   168→    value = raw.strip()
   169→    path = Path(value)
   170→    if (
   171→        not value
   172→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   173→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   174→    ):
   175→        raise ValueError(
   176→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   177→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   178→        )
   179→    return value
   180→
   181→
   182→def set_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   183→    """Parse and set a config value from a raw string.
   184→
   185→    Handles special cases:
   186→    - "never" → 0 for age keys
   187→    - "true"/"false" for bools
   188→    """
   189→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   190→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   191→
   192→    schema = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key]
   193→
   194→    if schema.type is int:
   195→        if raw.lower() == "never":
   196→            config[key] = 0
   197→        else:
   198→            config[key] = int(raw)
   199→        if key == "target_strict_score":
   200→            target_strict_score, target_valid = _coerce_target_strict_score(config[key])
   201→            if not target_valid:
   202→                raise ValueError(
   203→                    f"Expected integer {MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}-{MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE} "
   204→                    f"for {key}, got: {raw}"
   205→                )
   206→            config[key] = target_strict_score
   207→    elif schema.type is bool:
   208→        if raw.lower() in ("true", "1", "yes"):
   209→            config[key] = True
   210→        elif raw.lower() in ("false", "0", "no"):
   211→            config[key] = False
   212→        else:
   213→            raise ValueError(f"Expected true/false for {key}, got: {raw}")
   214→    elif schema.type is str:
   215→        if key == "badge_path":
   216→            config[key] = _validate_badge_path(raw)
   217→        else:
   218→            config[key] = raw
   219→    elif schema.type is list:
   220→        # For list keys, append the value
   221→        config.setdefault(key, [])
   222→        if raw not in config[key]:
   223→            config[key].append(raw)
   224→    elif schema.type is dict:
   225→        raise ValueError(f"Cannot set dict key '{key}' via CLI — use subcommands")
   226→    else:
   227→        config[key] = raw
   228→
   229→
   230→def unset_config_value(config: dict, key: str) -> None:
   231→    """Reset a config key to its default value."""
   232→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   233→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   234→    config[key] = copy.deepcopy(CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default)
   235→
   236→
   237→def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238→    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
   239→    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   240→
   241→
   242→def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
   243→    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
   244→    if key not in config:
   245→        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
   246→        return
   247→    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
   248→        for item in value:
   249→            if item not in config[key]:
   250→                config[key].append(item)
   251→        return
   252→    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
   253→        for dk, dv in value.items():
   254→            if dk not in config[key]:
   255→                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→def _migrate_from_state_files(config_path: Path) -> dict:
   259→    """Migrate config keys from state-*.json files into config.json.
   260→
   261→    Reads state["config"] from all state files, merges them (union for
   262→    lists, merge for dicts), writes config.json, and strips "config" from
   263→    the state files.
   264→    """
   265→    config: dict = {}
   266→    state_dir = config_path.parent
   267→    if not state_dir.exists():
   268→        return config
   269→
   270→    state_files = list(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")) + list(
   271→        state_dir.glob("state.json")
   272→    )
   273→    migrated_any = False
   274→    for sf in state_files:
   275→        try:
   276→            state_data = json.loads(sf.read_text())
   277→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   278→            logger.debug("Skipping unreadable state file %s: %s", sf, exc)
   279→            continue
   280→
   281→        old_config = state_data.get("config")
   282→        if not old_config or not isinstance(old_config, dict):
   283→            continue
   284→
   285→        # Merge: union for lists, merge for dicts, first-wins for scalars
   286→        for k, v in old_config.items():
   287→            if k not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   288→                continue
   289→            _merge_config_value(config, k, v)
   290→
   291→        # Strip "config" from state file
   292→        if "config" in state_data:
   293→            del state_data["config"]
   294→            try:
   295→                safe_write_text(sf, json.dumps(state_data, indent=2) + "\n")
   296→            except OSError as exc:
   297→                log_best_effort_failure(
   298→                    logger,
   299→                    f"rewrite state file {sf} after config migration",
   300→                    exc,
   301→                )
   302→
   303→        migrated_any = True
   304→
   305→    if migrated_any and config:
   306→        try:
   307→            save_config(config, config_path)
   308→        except OSError as exc:
   309→            log_best_effort_failure(
   310→                logger, f"save migrated config to {config_path}", exc
   311→            )
   312→
   313→    return config
   314→
```

```
    50→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):

    54→def _state_subjective_assessments(

    71→    if capabilities is None:

    73→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
    74→        return normalized
    75→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
    76→        return capabilities
    77→    raise ScanStateContractError(

   101→@dataclass
   102→class ScanRuntime:
   103→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   104→
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace
   106→    state_path: Path | None
   107→    state: state_mod.StateModel
   108→    path: Path

   115→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0
   116→    expired_manual_override_count: int = 0
   117→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   118→

   122→    """State merge outputs and previous score snapshots."""
   123→
   124→    diff: dict[str, object]
   125→    prev_overall: float | None
   126→    prev_objective: float | None
   127→    prev_strict: float | None
   128→    prev_verified: float | None
   129→    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]
   130→

   133→class ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   134→    """Noise budget settings and hidden finding counts for this scan."""
   135→
   136→    noise_budget: int
   137→    global_noise_budget: int
   138→    budget_warning: str | None
   139→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   140→    hidden_total: int

   142→
   143→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   144→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   145→    config: dict[str, object],
   146→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   147→    lang: LangRun | None,
   148→) -> LangRun | None:
   149→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   150→    if not lang:
   151→        return None
   152→
   153→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   154→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   155→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   156→        lang,
   157→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   158→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   159→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   160→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   161→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   162→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   163→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   164→        ),
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   168→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   169→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   170→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   171→    }
   172→    return runtime_lang

   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   181→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)
   211→
   212→
   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired
   238→
   239→
   240→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   241→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   242→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   243→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   244→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   245→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   246→    path = Path(args.path)
   247→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   248→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   249→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   250→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   251→        state
   252→    )
   253→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   254→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   255→            state,
   256→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,
   275→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   276→        profile=profile,
   277→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   278→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   279→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   280→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   281→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   350→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   351→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   352→    )
   353→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   354→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   355→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   356→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   357→    )
   358→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   359→        findings,
   360→        runtime,
   361→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   362→    )
   363→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   364→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   365→
   366→
   367→def merge_scan_results(
   368→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   369→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   370→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   371→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   377→
   378→    if not path_changed:
   379→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   380→    else:
   381→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   382→    prev_dim_scores = (
   383→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→
   390→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,
   394→        findings,
   395→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   409→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   410→    )
   411→    state_mod.save_state(
   412→        runtime.state,
   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    return ScanMergeResult(
   418→        diff=diff,
   419→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   420→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   421→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   422→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   423→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   424→    )
   425→
   426→
   427→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   428→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   429→    config: dict[str, object],
   430→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   431→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   432→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   433→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   434→    )
   435→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   436→    open_findings = [
   437→        finding
   438→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   439→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   440→        ).values()
   441→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   442→    ]
   443→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   444→        open_findings,
   445→        budget=noise_budget,
   446→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   447→    )
   448→
   449→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   450→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   451→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   452→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   453→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   454→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def persist_reminder_history(
   459→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   460→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> None:
   462→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   463→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   464→        return
   465→
   466→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   467→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   468→    state_mod.save_state(
   469→        runtime.state,
   470→        runtime.state_path,
   471→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   472→    )
   473→
   474→
   475→__all__ = [
   476→    "ScanStateContractError",
   477→    "ScanMergeResult",
   478→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   479→    "ScanRuntime",
   480→    "merge_scan_results",
   481→    "persist_reminder_history",
   482→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   483→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   484→    "run_scan_generation",
   485→]
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for scan command orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS, read_file_text
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _audit_excluded_dirs(
    17→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    18→    scanned_files: list[str],
    19→    project_root: Path,
    20→) -> list[dict]:
    21→    """Check if any --exclude directory has zero references from scanned code.
    22→
    23→    Returns findings for directories that appear stale (no file references them).
    24→    """
    25→    if not exclusions:
    26→        return []
    27→
    28→    candidate_dirs = [
    29→        ex_dir
    30→        for ex_dir in exclusions
    31→        if ex_dir not in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS and (project_root / ex_dir).is_dir()
    32→    ]
    33→    if not candidate_dirs:
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    # Read each scanned file once and mark any exclusions observed in content.
    37→    unresolved = set(candidate_dirs)
    38→    for filepath in scanned_files:
    39→        if not unresolved:
    40→            break
    41→        abs_path = (
    42→            filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(project_root / filepath)
    43→        )
    44→        content = read_file_text(abs_path)
    45→        if content is None:
    46→            logger.debug(
    47→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while auditing exclusions", filepath
    48→            )
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        matched = {ex_dir for ex_dir in unresolved if ex_dir in content}
    52→        if matched:
    53→            unresolved.difference_update(matched)
    54→
    55→    stale_findings = []
    56→    for ex_dir in candidate_dirs:
    57→        if ex_dir not in unresolved:
    58→            continue
    59→        stale_findings.append(
    60→            state_mod.make_finding(
    61→                "stale_exclude",
    62→                ex_dir,
    63→                ex_dir,
    64→                tier=4,
    65→                confidence="low",
    66→                summary=(
    67→                    f"Excluded directory '{ex_dir}' has 0 references from scanned code — "
    68→                    "may be stale"
    69→                ),
    70→                detail={"directory": ex_dir, "references": 0},
    71→            )
    72→        )
    73→    return stale_findings
    74→
    75→
    76→def _collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path: Path) -> dict | None:
    77→    """Collect LOC/file/directory counts for the configured language."""
    78→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    79→        return None
    80→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    81→    total_loc = 0
    82→    dirs = set()
    83→    for filepath in files:
    84→        try:
    85→            total_loc += len(Path(filepath).read_text().splitlines())
    86→            dirs.add(str(Path(filepath).parent))
    87→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    88→            logger.debug(
    89→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while collecting scan metrics: %s",
    90→                filepath,
    91→                exc,
    92→            )
    93→    return {
    94→        "total_files": len(files),
    95→        "total_loc": total_loc,
    96→        "total_directories": len(dirs),
    97→    }
    98→
    99→
   100→def _resolve_scan_profile(profile: str | None, lang) -> str:
   101→    """Resolve effective scan profile from CLI and language defaults."""
   102→    if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   103→        return profile
   104→    default_profile = getattr(lang, "default_scan_profile", "full") if lang else "full"
   105→    if default_profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   106→        return default_profile
   107→    return "full"
   108→
   109→
   110→def _effective_include_slow(include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> bool:
   111→    """Determine whether slow phases should run for this profile."""
   112→    return include_slow and profile != "ci"
   113→
   114→
   115→def _format_hidden_by_detector(hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> str:
   116→    """Format hidden findings counts for terminal output."""
   117→    return ", ".join(f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items())
   118→
   119→
   120→def _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   121→    args, lang, path: Path, metrics: dict | None
   122→) -> None:
   123→    """Warn when user explicitly selected a language but scan found zero files."""
   124→    explicit_lang = getattr(args, "lang", None)
   125→    if not explicit_lang or not lang or not metrics:
   126→        return
   127→    if metrics.get("total_files", 0) > 0:
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    suggestion = " Omit `--lang` to auto-detect."
   131→    root = path if path.is_dir() else path.parent
   132→    try:
   133→        detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(root)
   134→    except ValueError as exc:
   135→        detected = None
   136→        logger.debug(
   137→            "Auto-detect failed while warning for explicit lang on %s: %s", root, exc
   138→        )
   139→    else:
   140→        if detected and detected != lang.name:
   141→            suggestion = (
   142→                f" Detected `{detected}` for this path — use `--lang {detected}` "
   143→                "or omit `--lang`."
   144→            )
   145→
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  ⚠ No {lang.name} source files found under `{path}`.{suggestion}",
   149→            "yellow",
   150→        )
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _format_delta(value: float, prev: float | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
   155→    """Return (delta_str, color) for a score change."""
   156→    delta = value - prev if prev is not None else 0
   157→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta != 0 else ""
   158→    color = "green" if delta > 0 else ("red" if delta < 0 else "dim")
   159→    return delta_str, color
   160→
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "_audit_excluded_dirs",
   164→    "_collect_codebase_metrics",
   165→    "_effective_include_slow",
   166→    "_format_delta",
   167→    "_format_hidden_by_detector",
   168→    "_resolve_scan_profile",
   169→    "_warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files",
   170→]
   171→
```

```
     2→

     4→
     5→import os

     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root

    18→# Directories that are never useful to scan — always pruned during traversal.
    19→DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS = frozenset(
    20→    {
    21→        "node_modules",
    22→        ".git",

    24→        ".venv*",

    44→def set_exclusions(patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    45→    """Set global exclusion patterns (called once from CLI at startup)."""
    46→    runtime = current_runtime_context()
    47→    runtime.exclusions = tuple(patterns)
    48→    runtime.source_file_cache.clear()
    49→
    50→
    51→def get_exclusions() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    52→    """Return current extra exclusion patterns."""
    53→    return current_runtime_context().exclusions
    54→
    55→
    56→def enable_file_cache() -> None:
    57→    """Enable scan-scoped file content cache."""
    58→    runtime = current_runtime_context()
    59→    runtime.file_text_cache.enable()
    60→    runtime.cache_enabled = True
    61→
    62→
    63→def disable_file_cache() -> None:
    64→    """Disable file content cache and free memory."""
    65→    runtime = current_runtime_context()
    66→    runtime.file_text_cache.disable()
    67→    runtime.cache_enabled = False
    68→
    69→
    70→def is_file_cache_enabled() -> bool:
    71→    """Return whether scan-scoped file cache is currently enabled."""
    72→    return current_runtime_context().cache_enabled
    73→
    74→
    75→def read_file_text(filepath: str) -> str | None:
    76→    """Read a file as text, with optional caching."""
    77→    return current_runtime_context().file_text_cache.read(filepath)
    78→
    79→
    80→def clear_source_file_cache_for_tests() -> None:
    81→    current_runtime_context().source_file_cache.clear()
    82→
    83→
    84→def _is_excluded_dir(name: str, rel_path: str, extra: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    85→    in_default_exclusions = name in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS or name.endswith(".egg-info")
    86→    is_virtualenv_dir = name.startswith(".venv") or name.startswith("venv")
    87→    matches_extra_exclusion = bool(
    88→        extra
    89→        and any(
    90→            matches_exclusion(rel_path, exclusion) or exclusion == name
    91→            for exclusion in extra
    92→        )
    93→    )
    94→    return in_default_exclusions or is_virtualenv_dir or matches_extra_exclusion
    95→
    96→
    97→def _find_source_files_cached(
    98→    path: str,
    99→    extensions: tuple[str, ...],
   100→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
   101→    extra_exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = (),
   102→) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   103→    """Cached file discovery using os.walk with traversal-time pruning."""
   104→    cache_key = (path, extensions, exclusions, extra_exclusions)
   105→    cache = current_runtime_context().source_file_cache
   106→    cached = cache.get(cache_key)
   107→    if cached is not None:
   108→        return cached
   109→
   110→    project_root = get_project_root()
   111→    root = Path(path)
   112→    if not root.is_absolute():
   113→        root = project_root / root
   114→    all_exclusions = (exclusions or ()) + extra_exclusions
   115→    ext_set = set(extensions)
   116→    files: list[str] = []
   117→    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
   118→        rel_dir = _normalize_path_separators(_safe_relpath(dirpath, project_root))
   119→        dirnames[:] = sorted(
   120→            d
   121→            for d in dirnames
   122→            if not _is_excluded_dir(d, rel_dir + "/" + d, all_exclusions)
   123→        )
   124→        for fname in filenames:
   125→            if any(fname.endswith(ext) for ext in ext_set):
   126→                full = os.path.join(dirpath, fname)
   127→                rel_file = _normalize_path_separators(_safe_relpath(full, project_root))
   128→                if all_exclusions and any(
   129→                    matches_exclusion(rel_file, ex) for ex in all_exclusions
   130→                ):
   131→                    continue
   132→                files.append(rel_file)
   133→    result = tuple(sorted(files))
   134→    cache.put(cache_key, result)
   135→    return result
   136→
   137→
   138→def find_source_files(
   139→    path: str | Path, extensions: list[str], exclusions: list[str] | None = None
   140→) -> list[str]:
   141→    """Find all files with given extensions under a path, excluding patterns."""
   142→    return list(
   143→        _find_source_files_cached(
   144→            str(path),
   145→            tuple(extensions),
   146→            tuple(exclusions) if exclusions else None,
   147→            get_exclusions(),
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→
   151→
   152→def find_ts_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   153→    return find_source_files(path, [".ts", ".tsx"])
   154→
   155→
   156→def find_tsx_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   157→    return find_source_files(path, [".tsx"])
   158→
   159→
   160→def find_py_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   161→    return find_source_files(path, [".py"])
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS",
   166→    "set_exclusions",
   167→    "get_exclusions",
   168→    "enable_file_cache",
   169→    "disable_file_cache",
   170→    "is_file_cache_enabled",
   171→    "read_file_text",
   172→    "clear_source_file_cache_for_tests",
   173→    "find_source_files",
   174→    "find_ts_files",
   175→    "find_tsx_files",
   176→    "find_py_files",
   177→]
   178→
```

```
1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.state import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   151→    )
   152→
```

```
    10→def compute_max_params(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    11→    """Find the function with the most parameters. Returns (count, label) or None."""
    12→    def_re = re.compile(r"def\s+\w+\s*\(", re.MULTILINE)
    13→    max_params = 0
    14→    for m in def_re.finditer(content):
    15→        # Track paren depth to find matching close-paren (handles nested parens)
    16→        depth = 1
    17→        start = m.end()
    18→        i = start
    19→        while i < len(content) and depth > 0:
    20→            ch = content[i]
    21→            if ch == "(":
    22→                depth += 1
    23→            elif ch == ")":
    24→                depth -= 1
    25→            i += 1
    26→        if depth != 0:
    27→            continue
    28→        param_str = content[start : i - 1]
    29→        params = [p.strip() for p in param_str.split(",") if p.strip()]
    30→        real_params = [
    31→            p for p in params if p not in ("self", "cls") and not p.startswith("*")
    32→        ]
    33→        if len(real_params) > max_params:
    34→            max_params = len(real_params)
    35→    if max_params > 7:
    36→        return max_params, f"function with {max_params} params"
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _detect_indent_unit(lines: list[str]) -> int:
    41→    """Detect indentation unit from file content using GCD of indentations."""
    42→    indents = set()
    43→    for line in lines:
    44→        stripped = line.lstrip()
    45→        if stripped and not stripped.startswith("#"):
    46→            indent = len(line) - len(stripped)
    47→            if 0 < indent <= 16:
    48→                indents.add(indent)
    49→    if not indents:
    50→        return 4
    51→    unit = 0
    52→    for i in indents:
    53→        unit = gcd(unit, i)
    54→    return max(unit, 1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def compute_nesting_depth(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    58→    """Find maximum nesting depth by indentation. Returns (depth, label) or None."""
    59→    indent_unit = _detect_indent_unit(lines)
    60→    max_depth = 0
    61→    for line in lines:
    62→        stripped = line.lstrip()
    63→        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
    64→            continue
    65→        indent = len(line) - len(stripped)
    66→        depth = indent // indent_unit
    67→        if depth > max_depth:
    68→            max_depth = depth
    69→    if max_depth > 4:
    70→        return max_depth, f"nesting depth {max_depth}"
    71→    return None
    72→
    73→
    74→def compute_long_functions(content: str, lines: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    75→    """Find functions >80 LOC. Returns (longest_loc, label) or None."""
    76→    results = []
    77→    fn_re = re.compile(r"^(\s*)def\s+(\w+)")
    78→
    79→    i = 0
    80→    while i < len(lines):
    81→        m = fn_re.match(lines[i])
    82→        if not m:
    83→            i += 1
    84→            continue
    85→
    86→        fn_indent = len(m.group(1))
    87→        fn_name = m.group(2)
    88→        fn_start = i
    89→
    90→        j = i + 1
    91→        while j < len(lines):
    92→            if lines[j].strip() == "":
    93→                j += 1
    94→                continue
    95→            line_indent = len(lines[j]) - len(lines[j].lstrip())
    96→            if line_indent <= fn_indent and lines[j].strip():
    97→                break
    98→            j += 1
    99→
   100→        fn_loc = j - fn_start
   101→        if fn_loc > 80:
   102→            results.append((fn_name, fn_loc))
   103→        i = j
   104→
   105→    if results:
   106→        longest = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
   107→        return longest[1], f"long function ({longest[0]}: {longest[1]} LOC)"
   108→    return None
   109→
```

```
194→def _detect_ast_smells(filepath: str, content: str, smell_counts: dict[str, list]):
   195→    """Detect AST-based code smells using registry-driven collector dispatch."""
```
