```
   190→def _build_string_line_set(lines: list[str]) -> set[int]:
   191→    """Build a set of 0-indexed line numbers that are inside multi-line strings.
   192→
   193→    Tracks triple-quote state across lines so regex-based checks can skip
   194→    lines that are inside multi-line string literals.
   195→    """
   196→    in_multiline: str | None = None  # '"""' or "'''" or None
   197→    string_lines: set[int] = set()
   198→
   199→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   200→        if in_multiline is not None:
   201→            string_lines.add(i)
   202→            # Check if this line closes the multi-line string
   203→            if in_multiline in line:
   204→                # Find the closing triple-quote (skip escaped ones)
   205→                pos = 0
   206→                while pos < len(line):
   207→                    idx = line.find(in_multiline, pos)
   208→                    if idx == -1:
   209→                        break
   210→                    # Check it's not escaped
   211→                    backslashes = 0
   212→                    j = idx - 1
   213→                    while j >= 0 and line[j] == "\\":
   214→                        backslashes += 1
   215→                        j -= 1
   216→                    if backslashes % 2 == 0:
   217→                        in_multiline = None
   218→                        break
   219→                    pos = idx + 3
   220→            continue
   221→
   222→        # Check if this line opens a multi-line string
   223→        pos = 0
   224→        while pos < len(line):
   225→            ch = line[pos]
   226→            if ch == "#":
   227→                break  # Rest is comment
   228→            # Skip string prefixes
   229→            if ch in ("r", "b", "f", "u", "R", "B", "F", "U") and pos + 1 < len(line):
   230→                next_ch = line[pos + 1]
   231→                if next_ch in ('"', "'"):
   232→                    pos += 1
   233→                    ch = next_ch
   234→                elif (
   235→                    next_ch in ("r", "b", "f", "R", "B", "F")
   236→                    and pos + 2 < len(line)
   237→                    and line[pos + 2] in ('"', "'")
   238→                ):
   239→                    pos += 2
   240→                    ch = line[pos]
   241→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
   242→                triple = line[pos : pos + 3]
   243→                if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
   244→                    # Check if it closes on the same line
   245→                    close_idx = line.find(triple, pos + 3)
   246→                    if close_idx == -1:
   247→                        # Opens a multi-line string
   248→                        in_multiline = triple
   249→                        break
   250→                    else:
   251→                        pos = close_idx + 3
   252→                        continue
   253→                else:
   254→                    # Single-line string — skip to closing quote
   255→                    end = line.find(ch, pos + 1)
   256→                    while end != -1 and end > 0 and line[end - 1] == "\\":
   257→                        end = line.find(ch, end + 1)
   258→                    pos = (end + 1) if end != -1 else len(line)
   259→                    continue
   260→            pos += 1
   261→
   262→    return string_lines
   263→
   264→
   265→def _match_is_in_string(line: str, match_start: int) -> bool:
   266→    """Check if a regex match position falls inside a string literal or comment on a single line."""
   267→    i, in_string = 0, None
   268→    while i < len(line):
   269→        if i == match_start:
   270→            return in_string is not None
   271→        ch = line[i]
   272→        if in_string is None:
   273→            if ch == "#":
   274→                return True  # In a comment, not real code
   275→            triple = line[i : i + 3]
   276→            if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
   277→                in_string = triple
   278→                i += 3
   279→                continue
   280→            if (
   281→                ch in ("r", "b", "f")
   282→                and i + 1 < len(line)
   283→                and line[i + 1] in ('"', "'")
   284→            ):
   285→                i += 1
   286→                ch = line[i]
   287→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
   288→                in_string = ch
   289→                i += 1
   290→                continue
   291→        else:
   292→            if ch == "\\" and i + 1 < len(line):
   293→                i += 2
   294→                continue
   295→            if in_string in ('"""', "'''"):
   296→                if line[i : i + 3] == in_string:
   297→                    in_string = None
   298→                    i += 3
   299→                    continue
   300→            elif ch == in_string:
   301→                in_string = None
   302→                i += 1
   303→                continue
   304→        i += 1
   305→    return in_string is not None
   306→
   307→
   308→def detect_smells(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   309→    """Detect Python code smell patterns. Returns (entries, total_files_checked)."""
   310→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {s["id"]: [] for s in SMELL_CHECKS}
   311→    files = find_py_files(path)
   312→    # Collect module-level constants for cross-file duplicate detection
   313→    constants_by_key: dict[tuple[str, str], list[tuple[str, int]]] = {}
   314→
   315→    for filepath in files:
   316→        try:
   317→            p = (
   318→                Path(filepath)
   319→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   320→                else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   321→            )
   322→            content = p.read_text()
   323→            lines = content.splitlines()
   324→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   325→            log_best_effort_failure(
   326→                logger, f"read Python file for smell scan {filepath}", exc
   327→            )
   328→            continue
   329→
   330→        if _is_test_path(filepath):
   331→            continue
   332→
   333→        # Build set of lines inside multi-line strings to avoid false positives
   334→        multiline_string_lines = _build_string_line_set(lines)
   335→
   336→        for check in SMELL_CHECKS:
   337→            if check["pattern"] is None:
   338→                continue
   339→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   340→                # Skip lines inside multi-line strings
   341→                if i in multiline_string_lines:
   342→                    continue
   343→                m = re.search(check["pattern"], line)
   344→                if m and not _match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   345→                    # Skip URLs assigned to module-level constants (UPPER_CASE = "https://...")
   346→                    if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   347→                        r"^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=", line.strip()
   348→                    ):
   349→                        continue
   350→                    smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   351→                        {
   352→                            "file": filepath,
   353→                            "line": i + 1,
   354→                            "content": line.strip()[:100],
   355→                        }
   356→                    )
   357→
   358→        _detect_empty_except(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   359→        _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   360→        detect_ast_smells(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   361→        detect_star_import_no_all(filepath, content, path, smell_counts)
   362→        detect_vestigial_parameter(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   363→        collect_module_constants(filepath, content, constants_by_key)
   364→
   365→    # Cross-file: detect duplicate constants
   366→    detect_duplicate_constants(constants_by_key, smell_counts)
   367→
   368→    severity_order = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
   369→    entries = []
   370→    for check in SMELL_CHECKS:
   371→        matches = smell_counts[check["id"]]
   372→        if matches:
   373→            entries.append(
   374→                {
   375→                    "id": check["id"],
   376→                    "label": check["label"],
   377→                    "severity": check["severity"],
   378→                    "count": len(matches),
   379→                    "files": len(set(m["file"] for m in matches)),
   380→                    "matches": matches[:50],
   381→                }
   382→            )
   383→    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (severity_order.get(e["severity"], 9), -e["count"]))
   384→    return entries, len(files)

   386→
   387→def _walk_except_blocks(lines: list[str]):
   388→    """Yield (line_index, except_line_stripped, body_lines) for each except block."""
   389→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   390→        stripped = line.strip()
   391→        if not re.match(r"except\s*(?:\w|\(|:)", stripped) and stripped != "except:":
   392→            continue
   393→        if not stripped.endswith(":"):
   394→            continue
   395→        indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())
   396→        j, body_lines = i + 1, []
   397→        while j < len(lines):
   398→            next_line = lines[j]
   399→            next_stripped = next_line.strip()
   400→            if next_stripped == "":
   401→                j += 1
   402→                continue
   403→            if len(next_line) - len(next_line.lstrip()) <= indent:
   404→                break
   405→            body_lines.append(next_stripped)
   406→            j += 1
   407→        yield i, stripped, body_lines
   408→
   409→
   410→def _is_broad_except(stripped: str) -> bool:
   411→    """Check if except clause catches broadly (bare, Exception, BaseException)."""
   412→    if stripped == "except:":
   413→        return True
   414→    m = re.match(r"except\s+(\w+)", stripped)
   415→    return bool(m and m.group(1) in ("Exception", "BaseException"))
   416→
   417→
   418→def _detect_empty_except(
   419→    filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list]
   420→):
   421→    """Find broad except blocks that just pass or have empty body."""
   422→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   423→        if (not body_lines or body_lines == ["pass"]) and _is_broad_except(stripped):
   424→            smell_counts["empty_except"].append(
   425→                {
   426→                    "file": filepath,
   427→                    "line": i + 1,
   428→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   429→                }
   430→            )
   431→
   432→
   433→def _detect_swallowed_errors(
   434→    filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list]
   435→):
   436→    """Find except blocks that only print/log the error."""
   437→    _LOG_RE = r"(?:print|logging\.\w+|logger\.\w+|log\.\w+)\s*\("
   438→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   439→        if body_lines and all(re.match(_LOG_RE, s) for s in body_lines):
   440→            smell_counts["swallowed_error"].append(
   441→                {
   442→                    "file": filepath,
   443→                    "line": i + 1,
   444→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   445→                }
   446→            )
   447→
```

```
    21→
    22→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    25→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    26→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    27→
    28→
    29→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    30→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    31→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    32→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    33→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    34→
    35→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    36→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    37→
    38→def create_parser():
    39→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    40→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    41→
    42→
    43→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    44→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    45→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    46→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    47→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    48→    if not combined:
    49→        return
    50→    set_exclusions(combined)
    51→    if cli_exclusions:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    54→            file=sys.stderr,
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    print(
    58→        colorize(
    59→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    60→        ),
    61→        file=sys.stderr,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    66→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    67→
    68→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    69→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    70→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    71→    """
    72→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    73→        return
    74→    runtime_root = (
    75→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    76→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    77→        else get_project_root()
    78→    )
    79→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    80→        try:
    81→            state_file = state_path(args)
    82→            if state_file:
    83→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    84→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    85→                if saved_path:
    86→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    87→                    return
    88→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    89→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    90→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    91→    if lang:
    92→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    93→    else:
    94→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    95→
    96→
    97→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    98→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    99→    config = load_config()
   100→
   101→    state_file = state_path(args)
   102→    state = load_state(state_file)
   103→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   104→
   105→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   109→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   110→
   111→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   115→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   116→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   117→    try:
   118→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   119→    except SystemExit:
   120→        return
   121→
   122→
   123→def main() -> None:
   124→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   125→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   126→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   127→            try:
   128→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   129→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   130→                logger.debug(
   131→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   132→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   133→                )
   134→
   135→    parser = create_parser()
   136→    args = parser.parse_args()
   137→    if args.command == "help":
   138→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    try:
   142→        with runtime_scope():
   143→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   144→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   145→
   146→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   147→            handler(args)
   148→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   149→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   150→        sys.exit(1)
```

```
    25→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    26→    """Run file/code smell detectors plus cross-file signature variance."""
    27→    entries, total_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
    28→    # Supplement with ruff B/E/W rules not covered by the regex smells above.
    29→    ruff_entries = detect_with_ruff_smells(path)
    30→    if ruff_entries:
    31→        entries = entries + ruff_entries
    32→    results = make_smell_findings(entries, log)
    33→
    34→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path) if lang.extract_functions else []
    35→    sig_entries, _ = signature_detector_mod.detect_signature_variance(functions)
    36→    for entry in sig_entries:
    37→        results.append(
    38→            state_mod.make_finding(
    39→                "smells",
    40→                entry["files"][0],
    41→                f"sig_variance::{entry['name']}",
    42→                tier=3,
    43→                confidence="medium",
    44→                summary=(
    45→                    f"Signature variance: {entry['name']}() has {entry['signature_count']} "
    46→                    f"different signatures across {entry['file_count']} files"
    47→                ),
    48→                detail={
    49→                    "function": entry["name"],
    50→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
    51→                    "signature_count": entry["signature_count"],
    52→                    "variants": entry["variants"][:5],
    53→                },
    54→            )
    55→        )
    56→    if sig_entries:
    57→        log(
    58→            f"         signature variance: {len(sig_entries)} functions with inconsistent signatures"
    59→        )
    60→
    61→    return results, {
    62→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    63→    }
```

```
308→def detect_smells(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   309→    """Detect Python code smell patterns. Returns (entries, total_files_checked)."""
   310→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {s["id"]: [] for s in SMELL_CHECKS}
   311→    files = find_py_files(path)
   312→    # Collect module-level constants for cross-file duplicate detection
   313→    constants_by_key: dict[tuple[str, str], list[tuple[str, int]]] = {}
   314→
   315→    for filepath in files:
   316→        try:
   317→            p = (
   318→                Path(filepath)
   319→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   320→                else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   321→            )
   322→            content = p.read_text()
   323→            lines = content.splitlines()
   324→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   325→            log_best_effort_failure(
   326→                logger, f"read Python file for smell scan {filepath}", exc
   327→            )
   328→            continue
   329→
   330→        if _is_test_path(filepath):
   331→            continue
   332→
   333→        # Build set of lines inside multi-line strings to avoid false positives
   334→        multiline_string_lines = _build_string_line_set(lines)
   335→
   336→        for check in SMELL_CHECKS:
   337→            if check["pattern"] is None:
   338→                continue
   339→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   340→                # Skip lines inside multi-line strings
   341→                if i in multiline_string_lines:
   342→                    continue
   343→                m = re.search(check["pattern"], line)
   344→                if m and not _match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   345→                    # Skip URLs assigned to module-level constants (UPPER_CASE = "https://...")
   346→                    if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   347→                        r"^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=", line.strip()
   348→                    ):
   349→                        continue
   350→                    smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   351→                        {
   352→                            "file": filepath,
   353→                            "line": i + 1,
   354→                            "content": line.strip()[:100],
   355→                        }
   356→                    )
   357→
   358→        _detect_empty_except(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   359→        _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   360→        detect_ast_smells(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   361→        detect_star_import_no_all(filepath, content, path, smell_counts)
   362→        detect_vestigial_parameter(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   363→        collect_module_constants(filepath, content, constants_by_key)
```

## Summary

I've thoroughly traced desloppify's scan flow and identified how file exclusions work, plus why excluded files might still produce SyntaxWarnings. Here's what I found:

### 1. **How File Exclusions Work**

**Config Loading (Early Stage)**:
- `desloppify/cli.py` line 43-50: `_apply_persisted_exclusions()` reads from:
  - CLI `--exclude` flags (if provided)
  - Persisted `config.exclude` list from `.desloppify/config.json`
  - Merges them and calls `set_exclusions(combined)` to activate globally

**File Discovery (Filtering Stage)**:
- `desloppify/core/source_discovery.py` lines 44-53:
  - `set_exclusions()` stores patterns in runtime context
  - `get_exclusions()` retrieves them during scan
  
- `desloppify/core/source_discovery.py` lines 97-149:
  - `_find_source_files_cached()` uses `os.walk()` with directory pruning
  - For each file found, checks:
    1. Directory exclusion at traversal time (line 122): `_is_excluded_dir()` 
    2. File-level exclusion after discovery (lines 128-131): `matches_exclusion()`
  - `find_py_files()` calls this with `get_exclusions()` as the `extra_exclusions` parameter
  - Results are cached by the cache key including exclusions

**Exclusion Matching** (`desloppify/core/file_paths.py`):
- `matches_exclusion()` does path-component matching (not simple substring)
- Example: `Wan2GP/**` matches any file under the `Wan2GP` directory

### 2. **Where Files Get Parsed and SyntaxWarnings Occur**

**Main Flow**:
```
scan command → prepare_scan_runtime() → run_scan_generation()
  ↓
plan_mod.generate_findings()
  ↓
phase_smells() (in phases_quality.py:25)
  ↓
detect_smells() (detectors/smells.py:308)
  ↓
find_py_files(path) [Line 311] ← EXCLUSIONS APPLIED HERE
  ↓
for filepath in files:
  - p.read_text() [Line 322]
  - detect_ast_smells(filepath, content, smell_counts) [Line 360]
    ↓
    _detect_ast_smells() in _dispatch.py:194
      ↓
      ast.parse(content) [Line 197] ← PARSE WITH EXCLUSIONS ALREADY APPLIED
```

**Multiple AST Parse Calls**:
There are several places where `ast.parse(content)` is called on the file content:
1. `/desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/_dispatch.py:197` - Main smell detection
2. `/desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/_source_detectors.py:33` - Module constants collection
3. `/desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/_source_detectors.py:100` - Star import checking
4. Plus others in detectors (`unused.py`, `dict_keys.py`, etc.)

### 3. **Why Excluded Files Might Still Produce SyntaxWarnings**

**If it's happening, the likely causes are**:

**A. Exclusions not configured**:
   - If `exclude` list is empty in `.desloppify/config.json`, files won't be excluded
   - Check that config has: `"exclude": ["Wan2GP/**", ...]`

**B. Exclusion pattern mismatch**:
   - `matches_exclusion()` uses path-component matching
   - `Wan2GP/**` should match `Wan2GP/models/ltx_video/schedulers/rf.py`
   - But ensure the pattern is correct

**C. SyntaxWarning captured from subprocess**:
   - Some detectors run external tools (ruff, import-linter)
   - These might read files independently and issue warnings
   - Check `desloppify/languages/python/detectors/ruff_smells.py`

**D. Timing of exclusion setup**:
   - `set_exclusions()` must be called BEFORE `detect_smells()`
   - The flow in `cli.py:103` calls `_apply_persisted_exclusions()` before `prepare_scan_runtime()`
   - This should work correctly

### 4. **Key Files for Understanding Exclusions**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `desloppify/cli.py:43-62` | Reads config + CLI args, calls `set_exclusions()` |
| `desloppify/core/config.py:16` | Defines `exclude` config key |
| `desloppify/core/source_discovery.py:44-149` | File discovery with exclusion filtering |
| `desloppify/core/file_paths.py` | `matches_exclusion()` implementation |
| `desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells.py:308-384` | `detect_smells()` that uses `find_py_files()` |
| `desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/_dispatch.py:194-217` | `_detect_ast_smells()` calls `ast.parse()` |

### 5. **Why SyntaxWarning Format Indicates File Was Parsed**

The warning format `file.py:320: SyntaxWarning: invalid escape sequence '\D'` comes from Python's AST parser, which:
- Occurs during `ast.parse(content)` or `compile(content, filename, ...)`
- Reports the actual filename and line number
- Shows the specific syntax issue

This means the file **was** read and parsed, which shouldn't happen if exclusions are working correctly.

### 6. **Recommendation to Debug**

To verify if exclusions are actually being applied:

1. Check `.desloppify/config.json` has `"exclude": ["Wan2GP/**"]`
2. Run: `python -c "from desloppify.core.discovery_api import find_py_files; files = find_py_files('.'); print([f for f in files if 'Wan2GP' in f])"`
3. Verify `Wan2GP/` files don't appear in the list
4. If they do, the exclusion pattern isn't matching - try `Wan2GP/*` or just `Wan2GP`

The exclusion filtering is applied in `_find_source_files_cached()` before any file content is read or parsed, so if the pattern is correctly configured, the SyntaxWarning shouldn't occur.

```
    24→    _print_resolve_summary,
    25→    _print_score_movement,
    26→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    27→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    28→)
    29→from .selection import (
    30→    ResolveQueryContext,
    31→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,

    33→    _show_attestation_requirement,
    34→    _validate_attestation,

    37→
    38→
    39→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    40→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    41→    try:
    42→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    43→    except OSError as exc:
    44→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    49→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    50→    try:
    51→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    52→    except OSError as exc:
    53→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    54→        sys.exit(1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    59→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    60→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    61→
    62→    state_file = state_path(args)
    63→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    64→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    65→        state,
    66→        args,
    67→        attestation=attestation,
    68→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    69→    )
    70→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    71→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    72→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    73→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    74→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    78→    if not all_resolved:
    79→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    89→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
    90→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    91→        state,
    92→        status=args.status,
    93→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
    94→    )
    95→    has_review_findings = any(
    96→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
    97→        for fid in all_resolved
    98→    )
    99→    _print_score_movement(
   100→        status=args.status,
   101→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   102→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   103→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   104→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   105→        state=state,
   106→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   107→    )
   108→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   109→        args=args,
   110→        state=state,
   111→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   112→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   116→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   117→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   118→        state,
   119→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   120→    )
   121→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   122→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   123→
   124→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   125→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   126→        ResolveQueryContext(
   127→            patterns=args.patterns,
   128→            status=args.status,
   129→            resolved=all_resolved,
   130→            next_command=next_command,
   131→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   132→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   133→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   134→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   135→            attestation=attestation,
   136→            narrative=narrative,
   137→            state=state,
   138→        )
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   143→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   144→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   145→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
   146→        _show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   147→        sys.exit(1)
   148→
   149→    state_file = state_path(args)
   150→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   151→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   152→
   153→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   154→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   155→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   156→
   157→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   158→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   159→        {
   160→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   161→            "command": "ignore",
   162→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   163→            "attestation": attestation,
   164→            "affected": removed,
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   168→
   169→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   170→    if removed:
   171→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   172→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   173→
   174→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   175→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   176→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   177→        state,
   178→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   179→    )
   180→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   181→    write_query(
   182→        {
   183→            "command": "ignore",
   184→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   185→            "removed": removed,
   186→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
   187→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
   188→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
   189→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   190→            "attestation": attestation,
   191→            "narrative": narrative,
   192→        }
   193→    )
```

```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def _validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str, attestation: str | None, example: str
    51→) -> None:
    52→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    53→    if not attestation:
    54→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    55→    elif missing:
    56→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    57→        _emit_warning(
    58→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    59→        )
    60→    _emit_warning(
    61→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    62→    )
    63→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    64→
    65→
    66→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    67→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    68→        _emit_warning(
    69→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    70→        )
    71→        sys.exit(1)
    72→    if args.status == "open":
    73→        return
    74→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
    75→        _show_attestation_requirement("Manual resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
    76→        sys.exit(1)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
    80→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
    81→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    82→
    83→
    84→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
    85→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
    86→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
    87→    for pattern in patterns:
    88→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
    89→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
    90→            if finding_id:
    91→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
    92→    return len(matched_ids)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
    96→    state: dict,
    97→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    98→    *,
    99→    attestation: str | None,
   100→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   101→) -> float:
   102→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   103→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   104→    if before is None:
   105→        return 0.0
   106→
   107→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   108→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   109→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   110→    if after is None:
   111→        return 0.0
   112→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> None:
   122→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   123→        return
   124→
   125→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   126→    if preview_count <= 10:
   127→        return
   128→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   132→        state,
   133→        args,
   134→        attestation=attestation,
   135→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   136→    )
   137→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   138→    if strict_delta > 0:
   139→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   140→    _emit_warning(
   141→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   142→    )
   143→    sys.exit(1)
   144→
```

```
    14→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    15→    state: dict,
    16→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    17→    *,
    18→    attestation: str | None,
    19→) -> list[str]:
    20→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    21→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    22→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    23→            state,
    24→            pattern,
    25→            args.status,
    26→            args.note,
    27→            attestation=attestation,
    28→        )
    29→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    30→    return all_resolved
    31→
    32→
    33→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    34→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    35→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    36→        payload,
    37→        context="resolve query payload update",
    38→    )

    40→
    41→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    42→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)

    45→            "command": "resolve",
    46→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    47→            "status": context.status,

    51→            "overall_score": scores.overall,

    55→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    56→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,

    58→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    59→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    60→            "narrative": context.narrative,
```

```
    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )

   121→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   122→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   123→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   124→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   125→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   126→
   127→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   128→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   129→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   130→
   131→    if last_raw > 0:
   132→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   133→        print(
   134→            colorize(
   135→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   136→                style,
   137→            )
   138→        )
   139→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   140→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   141→
   142→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   143→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   144→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   145→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→        print(
   148→            colorize(
   149→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   150→                "dim",
   151→            )
   152→        )

   154→
   155→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   156→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   157→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   158→        state,
   159→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   160→    )
   161→
   162→
   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name
   184→
   185→
   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts
   208→
   209→
   210→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   211→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   212→) -> None:
   213→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   214→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   215→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   216→        if not ds:
   217→            continue
   218→        score_val = ds["score"]
   219→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   220→        checks = ds["checks"]
   221→
   222→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   223→
   224→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   225→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   226→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   227→        print(
   228→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   229→        )
   230→
   231→
   232→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   233→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   234→    *,
   235→    lowest_name: str | None,
   236→    bar_len: int,
   237→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   238→) -> None:
   239→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   240→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   241→        return
   242→    print(
   243→        colorize(
   244→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   245→            "dim",
   246→        )
   247→    )
   248→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   249→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   250→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   251→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   252→        tier = 4
   253→
   254→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   255→
   256→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   257→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   258→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   259→        placeholder_tag = (
   260→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   261→        )
   262→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   263→        cli_keys = [
   264→            str(key).strip()
   265→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   266→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   267→        ]
   268→        if dim_key:
   269→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   270→        elif cli_keys:
   271→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   272→        else:
   273→            issue_count = 0
   274→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   275→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   276→        print(
   277→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   278→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   279→        )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   283→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   284→    print(
   285→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   286→    )
   287→    print(
   288→        colorize(
   289→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   290→            "dim",
   291→        )
   292→    )
   293→    stale_keys = [
   294→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   295→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   296→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   297→    ]
   298→    has_open = False
   299→    if state is not None:
   300→        has_open = any(
   301→            f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   302→            for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   303→        )
   304→    if stale_keys and not has_open:
   305→        n = len(stale_keys)
   306→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   307→        print(
   308→            colorize(
   309→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   310→                f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   311→                "yellow",
   312→            )
   313→        )
   314→
   315→
   316→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   317→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   318→    print()
   319→    bar_len = 20
   320→    print(
   321→        colorize(
   322→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   323→            "dim",
   324→        )
   325→    )
   326→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   327→
   328→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   329→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   330→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   331→
   332→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   333→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   334→        scorecard_subjective,
   335→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   336→        bar_len=bar_len,
   337→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   338→    )
   339→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   340→    print()
   341→
   342→
   343→def show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   344→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   345→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   346→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   347→    if not lowest_name:
   348→        return
   349→
   350→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   351→    lowest_kind = None
   352→    lowest_score = 101.0
   353→    lowest_issues = 0
   354→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   355→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   356→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   357→            if ds:
   358→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   359→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   360→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   361→            break
   362→    else:
   363→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   364→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   365→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   366→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   367→                lowest_issues = 0
   368→                break
   369→
   370→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   371→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   372→            print(
   373→                colorize(
   374→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   375→                    "cyan",
   376→                )
   377→            )
   378→            print()
   379→            return
   380→
   381→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   382→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   383→        if target_dim:
   384→            impact = 0.0
   385→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   386→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   387→                    {
   388→                        k: {
   389→                            "score": v["score"],
   390→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   391→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   392→                        }
   393→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   394→                        if "score" in v
   395→                    },
   396→                    potentials,
   397→                    det,
   398→                    lowest_issues,
   399→                )
   400→                if impact > 0:
   401→                    break
   402→
   403→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   404→            print(
   405→                colorize(
   406→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   407→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   408→                    "cyan",
   409→                )
   410→            )
   411→            print()
   412→
   413→

   416→) -> None:

   418→    if not dim_scores:

   454→
   455→def _collect_structural_areas(
   456→    state: dict,
   457→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   458→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   459→
   460→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   461→    """
   462→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   463→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   464→    )
   465→
   466→    structural = [
   467→        f
   468→        for f in findings.values()
   469→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   470→    ]
   471→
   472→    if len(structural) < 5:
   473→        return None
   474→
   475→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   476→    for f in structural:
   477→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   478→        areas[area].append(f)
   479→
   480→    if len(areas) < 2:
   481→        return None
   482→
   483→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   484→
   485→
   486→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   487→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   488→    rows = []
   489→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   490→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   491→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   492→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   493→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   494→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   495→        rows.append(
   496→            [
   497→                area,
   498→                str(len(area_findings)),
   499→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   500→                str(open_count),
   501→                str(debt_count),
   502→                str(weight),
   503→            ]
   504→        )
   505→    return rows
   506→
   507→
   508→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   509→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   510→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   511→    if remaining > 0:
   512→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   513→
   514→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   515→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   516→    print(
   517→        colorize(
   518→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   519→        )
   520→    )
   521→    print(
   522→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   523→    )
   524→    print()
   525→
   526→
   527→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   528→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   529→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   530→    if sorted_areas is None:
   531→        return
   532→
   533→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   534→    print(
   535→        colorize(
   536→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   537→            "dim",
   538→        )
   539→    )
   540→    print()
   541→
   542→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   543→    print_table(
   544→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   545→    )
   546→
   547→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   548→
   549→
   550→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   551→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   552→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   553→    review_open = [
   554→        f
   555→        for f in findings.values()
   556→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   557→    ]
   558→    if not review_open:
   559→        return
   560→    uninvestigated = sum(
   561→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   562→    )
   563→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   564→    if uninvestigated:
   565→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   566→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   567→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   568→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   569→        print(
   570→            colorize(
   571→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   572→                "dim",
   573→            )
   574→        )
   575→    print()
   576→
```

```
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )

   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'

   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:

   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}

   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)

   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:

   195→    return scores

   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
```

```
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→
```

```
   264→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:

   279→        return False
   280→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""

   284→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])

   286→            commands = " | ".join(

   346→def render_followup_nudges(
   347→    state: dict,
   348→    dim_scores: dict,
   349→    findings_scoped: dict,
   350→    *,
   351→    strict_score: float | None,
   352→    target_strict_score: float,
   353→) -> None:
   354→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   355→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   356→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   357→        state,
   358→        subjective_entries,
   359→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   360→        max_quality_items=3,
   361→        max_integrity_items=5,
   362→    )
   363→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   364→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   365→    if strict_score is not None:
   366→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   367→        if gap > 0:
   368→            print(
   369→                colorize(
   370→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   371→                    "cyan",
   372→                )
   373→            )
   374→        else:
   375→            print(
   376→                colorize(
   377→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   378→                    "green",
   379→                )
   380→            )
   381→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   382→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   383→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   384→
   385→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   386→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   387→        findings_scoped
   388→    )
   389→    parts: list[str] = []
   390→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   391→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
```
