1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if status == "wontfix":
    94→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
    95→        if strict_gap > 0:
    96→            print(
    97→                colorize(
    98→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    99→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        print(
   111→            colorize(
   112→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   113→                "dim",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    elif status == "fixed":
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   126→    *,
   127→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    all_resolved: list[str],
   130→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    has_review = any(
   133→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   134→        for fid in all_resolved
   135→    )
   136→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    stale_dims = sorted(
   140→        dim
   141→        for dim in {
   142→            str(
   143→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   144→            ).strip()
   145→            for fid in all_resolved
   146→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   147→        }
   148→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   149→    )
   150→    if not stale_dims:
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   154→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   155→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→    print(
   163→        colorize(
   164→            "  Next subjective step: "
   165→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   166→            "dim",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   172→    remaining = sum(
   173→        1
   174→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   175→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   176→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   177→    )
   178→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            colorize(
   183→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   184→                "dim",
   185→            )
   186→        )
   187→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   188→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   189→    print()
   190→    return next_command
   191→

1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "

    46→class TestCmdResolve:
    47→    """Test resolve command with mocked state layer."""
    48→
    49→    def test_wontfix_without_note_exits(self, monkeypatch):
    50→        """Wontfix without --note should exit with error."""
    51→        monkeypatch.setattr(resolve_mod, "state_path", lambda a: "/tmp/fake.json")
    52→

    55→            note = None

    60→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc_info:

    62→        assert exc_info.value.code == 1
    63→
    64→    def test_fixed_without_attestation_exits(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    65→        monkeypatch.setattr(resolve_mod, "state_path", lambda a: "/tmp/fake.json")
    66→
    67→        class FakeArgs:
    68→            status = "fixed"

    82→
    83→    def test_fixed_with_incomplete_attestation_exits(self, monkeypatch, capsys):

    41→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    51→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    52→
    53→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    54→        print(
    55→            json.dumps(

    60→                    scorecard_dims,
    61→                    subjective_measures,
    62→                    suppression,
    63→                ),
    64→                indent=2,
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→        return
    68→

    70→        return

    78→
    79→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    80→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})

    82→
    83→    lang = resolve_lang(args)

    85→    narrative = compute_narrative(

    99→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)

   118→
   119→    show_review_summary(state)
   120→    show_structural_areas(state)

   129→

   131→    if review_age != 30:
   132→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   133→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   134→    print()
   135→
   136→    write_status_query(
   137→        state=state,
   138→        stats=stats,
   139→        by_tier=by_tier,
   140→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   141→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
   142→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
   143→        suppression=suppression,
   144→        narrative=narrative,
   145→        ignores=ignores,
   146→        overall_score=scores.overall,

   148→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   149→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   150→    )

   152→
   153→def _status_json_payload(
   154→    state: dict,
   155→    stats: dict,
   156→    dim_scores: dict,
   157→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   158→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   159→    suppression: dict,
   160→) -> dict:
   161→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   162→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   163→    open_scope = (
   164→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   165→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   166→        else None
   167→    )
   168→    return {
   169→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   170→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   171→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   172→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   173→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   174→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   175→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   176→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   177→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   178→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   179→        "stats": stats,
   180→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   181→        "suppression": suppression,
   182→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   183→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   184→    }
   185→

1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    11→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    12→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    18→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    19→
    20→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    21→        return
    22→
    23→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    24→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    25→
    26→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    27→    if output:
    28→        try:
    29→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    30→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    31→            print_agent_plan(["Inspect and execute the generated plan."], next_command=next_command)
    32→        except OSError as e:
    33→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    34→    else:
    35→        print(plan_md)
    36→        print()
    37→        print_agent_plan(["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."], next_command=next_command)
    38→

    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""

    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):

    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])

## Comprehensive Report: Resolve/Attestation and Status/Stale Workflows

### 1. RESOLVE FIXED COMMAND & ATTESTATION WORKFLOW

#### 1.1 Entry Point
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`

- **`cmd_resolve(args)`** (line 57): Main resolve command handler
  - Retrieves attestation from `args.attest` (line 59)
  - Calls `_validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)` (line 60) — this is where the hard-fail happens
  - Proceeds only if validation passes
  - Attestation is logged in query output and passed to `state_mod.resolve_findings()`

#### 1.2 Attestation Validation & Error Display

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/selection.py`

**Constants (lines 14-15):**
```python
_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
```

**Validation Logic:**
- `_validate_attestation(attestation)` (line 45): Returns True only if both phrases are present (case-insensitive, whitespace-normalized)
- `_missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)` (line 38): Returns list of missing phrases
- `_validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)` (line 66): Hard-fail check for `status != "open"`
  - If `status == "wontfix"`: Requires `--note` (lines 67-71), then proceeds without attestation requirement
  - If `status != "open"` (i.e., "fixed" or "false_positive"): Requires valid attestation (lines 72-76)
  - Calls `_show_attestation_requirement()` on failure and exits with code 1

**Error Output (lines 49-63):**
```python
def _show_attestation_requirement(label: str, attestation: str | None, example: str) -> None:
    # Emits to stderr via colorize(..., "yellow")
    # Shows what's missing + required keywords + example
```

The flow:
1. If no `--attest` provided: `"{label} requires --attest."`
2. If incomplete: `"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."`
3. Always shows: `"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."` 
4. Always shows example: `'Example: --attest "{example}"'` (in dim color)

#### 1.3 ATTEST_EXAMPLE Template

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (lines 20-23)
```python
ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
)
```

**Where it's used:**
- Imported and used in `resolve/cmd.py` (lines 16, 146)
- Imported and used in `resolve/selection.py` (line 11, 75)
- Imported and used in `next_parts/render.py` (lines 12, 338, 342)
- Imported and used in `app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py` (line 16)

#### 1.4 Where Attestation is Shown in Next/Plan Output

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` (lines 313-343)

**Function:** `render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)`
- When rendering a single item, shows both `fixed` and `wontfix` resolution commands (lines 337-342)
- Displays both commands with full `--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"` parameter:
  ```
  desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"
  desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"
  ```

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (line 168)
- `primary_command_for_finding()` generates the command shown in work queue items
- For non-auto-fix, non-review findings: returns resolve command with `--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"`

### 2. STATUS COMMAND & STALE DISPLAY WORKFLOW

#### 2.1 What is "Stale"?

**Definition (File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` lines 103-107):
```python
stale = bool(
    dim_key
    and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
    and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
)
```

A subjective dimension is **stale** when:
- It has a `dimension_key` 
- There's a subjective assessment entry for that key
- That assessment has `needs_review_refresh = True` set

This is set by the review workflow when a finding is resolved in a dimension without re-reviewing that dimension.

#### 2.2 Where Stale is Rendered in Status Output

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py`

**In dimension table (line 258):**
```python
stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
```
- Renders as yellow `[stale]` tag next to subjective dimension name in the dimension health table
- Example: `Test health  ✓ [stale]` in the output

**In legend/footer (lines 293-313):**
```python
stale_keys = [
    str(e.get("dimension_key"))
    for e in scorecard_subjective
    if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
]
has_open = False
if state is not None:
    has_open = any(
        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
        for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
    )
if stale_keys and not has_open:
    n = len(stale_keys)
    dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
    print(colorize(
        f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
        f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
        "yellow",
    ))
```
- Shows a summary footer message when there are stale dimensions and NO open findings
- Lists the count of stale dimensions and provides the re-review command

#### 2.3 Where Stale is Computed & Propagated

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (lines 70-122)

Function: `scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=None)`
- Returns list of subjective dimension entries with `stale` flag set
- Called from:
  - `app/commands/status_parts/render.py` — for status display
  - `app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_dimensions.py` — for scan reporting
  - `app/commands/next_parts/render.py` — for next/plan output

#### 2.4 How Stale vs Open Findings are Distinguished in Subjective Output

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (lines 250-270)

Function: `build_subjective_items()` — Creates synthetic work queue items for subjective dimensions

- **Unassessed** (line 251-252): `placeholder=True` → `desloppify review --prepare`
- **Stale** (line 253-259): `needs_review_refresh=True` → `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {cli_keys}`
- **Regular** (line 260-268): Below threshold but not stale → show review issues or review command

The summary line includes a stale tag (line 269):
```python
stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
```

#### 2.5 Stale in Next/Plan Output

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` (lines 392, 400)

```python

# ...

    parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
```

In `render_followup_nudges()`:
- Counts stale dimensions and includes in subjective summary
- Shows as: `"Subjective: {low_dims} dimensions below target, {stale_count} stale, ..."`

---

### 3. KEY DATA FLOW & INTERCONNECTIONS

#### Attestation Flow:
```
cmd_resolve() 
  → _validate_resolve_inputs() [hard-fail location]
    → _validate_attestation() 
      → _missing_attestation_keywords()
  → _show_attestation_requirement() [error message location]
    → stderr output with keywords + example

render_single_item_resolution_hint()
  → displays ATTEST_EXAMPLE in suggested commands

primary_command_for_finding()
  → generates command with ATTEST_EXAMPLE
```

#### Stale Flow:
```
state.subjective_assessments[dim_key].needs_review_refresh = True
  → scorecard_subjective_entries() reads it
    → entry.get("stale") = True
      → status_parts/render.py displays [stale] tag
      → next_parts/render.py counts stale dimensions
      → build_subjective_items() marks item as stale

Finding resolution in dimension
  → marks that dimension with needs_review_refresh = True
  → next scan shows [stale] tag
  → suggests re-review command
```

---

### 4. KEY FILES SUMMARY

| File | Purpose | Key Functions/Lines |
|------|---------|-----|
| `resolve/cmd.py` | Main resolve handler | `cmd_resolve()`, `cmd_ignore_pattern()` |
| `resolve/selection.py` | Attestation validation | `_validate_attestation()`, `_show_attestation_requirement()`, `_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES` |
| `engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` | ATTEST_EXAMPLE definition & work queue | `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`, `primary_command_for_finding()`, `build_subjective_items()` |
| `status_parts/render.py` | Status display | `show_dimension_table()`, `_render_dimension_legend()` — stale tag rendering at line 258 |
| `engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` | Stale flag computation | `scorecard_subjective_entries()` — stale detection at lines 103-107 |
| `next_parts/render.py` | Next/plan output | `render_single_item_resolution_hint()`, `render_followup_nudges()` — attestation at lines 337-342, stale count at line 392 |
| `app/commands/next.py` | Next command entry | `cmd_next()`, `_get_items()` |
| `app/commands/status_cmd.py` | Status command entry | `cmd_status()` |
| `app/commands/plan_cmd.py` | Plan command entry | `cmd_plan_output()` |

---

### 5. SUGGESTED IMPROVEMENTS (Based on Current Architecture)

For **attestation UX:**
1. Error message already shows required format — currently emitted to stderr with example
2. Could enhance by showing it proactively in `next` output warning section
3. Could add a hint section in `status` output under "Technical Debt" when wontfix items exist

For **stale display:**
1. Currently shows `[stale]` in status dimension table (yellow tag)
2. Currently shows count in next output subjective summary
3. Could improve distinction by:
   - Showing `[open]` vs `[stale]` tags in same context for side-by-side comparison
   - Adding a separate "Stale Dimensions" section in status output
   - In next output, could separate stale items from unassessed items more visually

     2→

     6→import sys

    16→
    17→
    18→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    19→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    20→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    21→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    22→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    23→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    24→            continue
    25→        try:
    26→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    27→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→            continue
    29→
    30→
    31→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    33→    _enable_live_review_output()
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

    43→        )
    44→        sys.exit(1)
    45→
    46→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    47→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))

    49→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    50→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    51→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    52→
    53→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    54→    mode_flags = [
    55→        merge,
    56→        run_batches,
    57→        external_start,
    58→        external_submit,
    59→        import_mode,
    60→        bool(validate_import_file),
    61→    ]
    62→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:

    69→            file=sys.stderr,
    70→        )
    71→        sys.exit(1)
    72→
    73→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    74→        print(
    75→            colorize(
    76→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    77→                "red",
    78→            ),
    79→            file=sys.stderr,
    80→        )
    81→        sys.exit(2)
    82→
    83→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    84→        print(
    85→            colorize(
    86→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    87→                "red",
    88→            ),
    89→            file=sys.stderr,
    90→        )
    91→        sys.exit(2)
    92→
    93→    if merge:
    94→        from desloppify.app.commands.review.merge import do_merge
    95→
    96→        do_merge(args)
    97→        return
    98→
    99→    if run_batches:
   100→        _do_run_batches(
   101→            args,
   102→            state,
   103→            lang,
   104→            state_file,
   105→            config=runtime.config,
   106→        )
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if external_start:
   110→        do_external_start(
   111→            args,
   112→            state,
   113→            lang,
   114→            config=runtime.config,
   115→        )
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    if external_submit:
   119→        do_external_submit(
   120→            import_file=str(import_file),
   121→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   122→            state=state,
   123→            lang=lang,
   124→            state_file=state_file,
   125→            config=runtime.config,
   126→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   127→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   128→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   129→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   130→        )
   131→        return

1→"""Prepare flow for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.review import runtime as review_runtime_mod
    10→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_positive_int
    11→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    12→from desloppify.intelligence import review as review_mod
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES = 80
    16→
    17→
    18→def _redacted_review_config(config: dict | None) -> dict:
    19→    """Return review packet config with target score removed for blind assessment."""
    20→    if not isinstance(config, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    return {key: value for key, value in config.items() if key != "target_strict_score"}
    23→
    24→
    25→def _coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config: dict | None) -> int | None:
    26→    """Resolve per-batch review file cap from config (0/negative => unlimited)."""
    27→    raw = (config or {}).get("review_batch_max_files", DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES)
    28→    try:
    29→        value = int(raw)
    30→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    31→        return DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    32→    return value if value > 0 else None
    33→
    34→
    35→def do_prepare(
    36→    args,
    37→    state,
    38→    lang,
    39→    _state_path,
    40→    *,
    41→    config: dict,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    """Prepare mode: holistic-only review packet in query.json."""
    44→    path = Path(args.path)
    45→    dims_str = getattr(args, "dimensions", None)
    46→    dimensions = dims_str.split(",") if dims_str else None
    47→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    48→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    49→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    50→        default=30,
    51→    )
    52→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    53→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    54→        default=20,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    lang_run, found_files = review_runtime_mod.setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
    58→
    59→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    60→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    61→        state,
    62→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    63→    )
    64→    data = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    65→        path,
    66→        lang_run,
    67→        state,
    68→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    69→            dimensions=dimensions,
    70→            files=found_files or None,
    71→            max_files_per_batch=_coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    72→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    73→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    74→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    75→        ),
    76→    )
    77→    next_command = (
    78→        "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
    79→    )
    80→    if retrospective:
    81→        next_command += (
    82→            " --retrospective"
    83→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    84→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    85→        )
    86→    data["config"] = _redacted_review_config(config)
    87→    data["narrative"] = narrative
    88→    data["next_command"] = next_command
    89→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    90→    if total == 0:
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                f"\n  Error: no files found at path '{path}'. "
    94→                "Nothing to review.",
    95→                "red",
    96→            ),
    97→            file=sys.stderr,
    98→        )
    99→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
   100→        if scan_path:
   101→            print(
   102→                colorize(
   103→                    f"  Hint: your last scan used --path {scan_path}. "
   104→                    f"Try: desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}",
   105→                    "yellow",
   106→                ),
   107→                file=sys.stderr,
   108→            )
   109→        else:
   110→            print(

   112→                    "  Hint: pass --path <dir> matching the path used during scan.",
   113→                    "yellow",
   114→                ),
   115→                file=sys.stderr,
   116→            )
   117→        sys.exit(1)
   118→    write_query(data)
   119→    batches = data.get("investigation_batches", [])
   120→    print(colorize(f"\n  Holistic review prepared: {total} files in codebase", "bold"))
   121→    if retrospective:
   122→        print(
   123→            colorize(

   127→        )
   128→    if batches:

    88→    return value if value > 0 else None

    90→
    91→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    92→    """Deterministically merge assessments/findings across batch outputs."""
    93→    normalized_results: list[dict] = []
    94→    for result in batch_results:
    95→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    96→            payload = result.to_dict()
    97→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
    98→                normalized_results.append(payload)
    99→                continue
   100→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   101→            normalized_results.append(result)
   102→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
   103→        normalized_results,
   104→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   105→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   106→    )

   197→def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   198→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""

   215→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   216→        default=15.0,
   217→    )
   218→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   219→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   220→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   221→        else 5.0
   222→    )
   223→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = coerce_non_negative_int(
   224→        getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
   225→        default=CODEX_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS,
   226→    )

   255→            ),
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(

   265→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   266→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda args, *, batch_count: runner_helpers_mod.selected_batch_indexes(

   298→        "status": "open",
   299→        "runner": runner,
   300→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(now),
   301→        "expires_at": _iso_seconds(expires),
   302→        "ttl_hours": ttl_hours,
   303→        "token": token,
   304→        "attest": EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT,

   313→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   314→    safe_write_text(template_path, json.dumps(template_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   315→    safe_write_text(

   317→        _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   318→            session_id=session_id,
   319→            token=token,
   320→            blind_path=blind_path,
   321→            template_path=template_path,
   322→            output_path=output_path,
   323→        )
   324→        + "\n",
   325→    )
   326→
   327→    instructions = "\n".join(

   329→            "# External Blind Review Session",

   348→            "5. Use the blind packet only (no score targets or prior context).",
   349→        ]

   351→    safe_write_text(instructions_path, instructions + "\n")
   352→

   355→        f"--session-id {session_id} --import {output_path}"
   356→    )
   357→    submit_with_scan_cmd = f"{submit_cmd} --scan-after-import"
   358→    print(colorize("\n  External review session started.", "bold"))
   359→    print(colorize(f"  Runner: {runner}", "dim"))

   377→    print(colorize(f"  4. Optional auto-rescan: `{submit_with_scan_cmd}`", "dim"))
   378→
   379→
   380→def _canonical_external_payload(
   381→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   382→    *,
   383→    session: dict[str, Any],
   384→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   385→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   386→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   387→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   388→        print(
   389→            colorize(
   390→                "  Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object.",
   391→                "red",
   392→            ),
   393→            file=sys.stderr,
   394→        )
   395→        print(
   396→            colorize(
   397→                "  Expected: {\"session\":{\"id\":\"...\",\"token\":\"...\"},\"assessments\":{...},\"findings\":[...]}",
   398→                "dim",
   399→            ),
   400→            file=sys.stderr,
   401→        )
   402→        sys.exit(1)
   403→
   404→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   405→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   406→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   407→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   408→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   409→        print(
   410→            colorize(
   411→                "  Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload.",
   412→                "red",
   413→            ),
   414→            file=sys.stderr,
   415→        )
   416→        print(
   417→            colorize("  Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.", "yellow"),
   418→            file=sys.stderr,
   419→        )
   420→        sys.exit(1)
   421→
   422→    payload = dict(raw_payload)
   423→    payload.pop("session", None)
   424→    payload.pop("provenance", None)
   425→    payload["provenance"] = {
   426→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   427→        "blind": True,
   428→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   429→        "run_stamp": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   430→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   431→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   432→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   433→        "external_session_id": expected_id,
   434→    }
   435→    return payload
   436→
   437→
   438→def _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   439→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   440→    if status == "open":
   441→        return
   442→    print(
   443→        colorize(
   444→            f"  Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   445→            "red",
   446→        ),
   447→        file=sys.stderr,
   448→    )
   449→    sys.exit(1)
   450→
   451→
   452→def _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   453→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   454→    if expires_at is None:
   455→        print(
   456→            colorize(
   457→                "  Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   458→                "red",
   459→            ),
   460→            file=sys.stderr,
   461→        )
   462→        sys.exit(1)
   463→    now = _utc_now()
   464→    if now <= expires_at:
   465→        return
   466→    print(
   467→        colorize(
   468→            f"  Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   469→            "red",
   470→        ),
   471→        file=sys.stderr,
   472→    )
   473→    sys.exit(1)
   474→
   475→
   476→def do_external_submit(
   477→    *,
   478→    import_file: str,
   479→    session_id: str,
   480→    state: dict,
   481→    lang,
   482→    state_file,
   483→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   484→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   485→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   486→    scan_path: str = ".",
   487→    dry_run: bool = False,
   488→) -> None:
   489→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   490→    config = config or {}
   491→    session_path, session = _session_payload_or_exit(session_id)
   492→    _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session)
   493→    _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session)
   494→
   495→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   496→        print(
   497→            colorize(
   498→                "  Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   499→                "red",
   500→            ),
   501→            file=sys.stderr,
   502→        )
   503→        sys.exit(1)
   504→
   505→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   506→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(findings_path, label="external findings")
   507→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   508→
   509→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   510→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   511→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   512→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   513→
   514→    if dry_run:
   515→        do_validate_import(
   516→            str(canonical_path),
   517→            lang,
   518→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   519→            attested_external=True,
   520→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   521→        )
   522→        return
   523→
   524→    do_import(
   525→        str(canonical_path),
   526→        state,
   527→        lang,
   528→        state_file,
   529→        config=config,
   530→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   531→        attested_external=True,
   532→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   536→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   537→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   538→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(findings_path)
   539→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   540→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   541→
   542→    if scan_after_import:
   543→        from .batch import FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
   544→
   545→        code = runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   546→            lang_name=lang.name,
   547→            scan_path=scan_path,
   548→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   549→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   550→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   551→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   552→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   553→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   554→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   555→            ),
   556→        )
   557→        if code != 0:
   558→            raise SystemExit(code)
   559→
   560→
   561→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   562→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→
    40→
    41→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    42→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    43→
    44→    items: list[dict]
    45→    total: int
    46→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    47→    requested_tier: int | None
    48→    selected_tier: int | None
    49→    fallback_reason: str | None
    50→    available_tiers: list[int]
    51→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    52→
    53→
    54→def build_work_queue(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    58→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    59→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    60→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    61→
    62→    status = resolved_options.status
    63→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    64→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    65→    try:
    66→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    67→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    68→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    69→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    70→
    71→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    72→        state,
    73→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    74→        status_filter=status,
    75→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    76→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    77→    )
    78→
    79→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    80→    if (
    81→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    82→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    83→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    84→    ):
    85→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    86→            state,
    87→            state.get("findings", {}),
    88→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
    89→        )
    90→        for item in subjective_items:
    91→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
    92→                all_items.append(item)
    93→
    94→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    95→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
    96→
    97→    requested_tier = (
    98→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
    99→    )
   100→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   101→    fallback_reason = None
   102→    filtered = all_items
   103→
   104→    if requested_tier is not None:
   105→        filtered = [
   106→            item
   107→            for item in all_items
   108→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   109→        ]
   110→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   111→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   112→            if chosen is not None:
   113→                selected_tier = chosen
   114→                filtered = [
   115→                    item
   116→                    for item in all_items
   117→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   118→                ]
   119→                fallback_reason = (
   120→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   121→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   122→                )
   123→        elif not filtered:
   124→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   125→
   126→    total = len(filtered)
   127→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   128→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   129→
   130→    if resolved_options.explain:
   131→        for item in filtered:
   132→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   133→
   134→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   135→    return {
   136→        "items": filtered,
   137→        "total": total,
   138→        "tier_counts": counts,
   139→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   140→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   141→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   142→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   144→    }
   145→
   146→
   147→__all__ = [
   148→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   149→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   150→    "WorkQueueResult",
   151→    "build_work_queue",
   152→    "group_queue_items",
   153→]
   154→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→__all__ = [
   297→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   298→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   299→    "build_subjective_items",
   300→    "detail_dict",
   301→    "is_review_finding",
   302→    "is_subjective_finding",
   303→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   304→    "review_finding_weight",
   305→    "scope_matches",
   306→    "slugify",
   307→    "status_matches",
   308→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   309→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   310→]
   311→

    16→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→
    89→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    90→
    91→    queue = build_work_queue(
    92→        state,
    93→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    94→            tier=tier,
    95→            count=count,
    96→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    97→            scope=scope,
    98→            status=status,
    99→            include_subjective=True,
   100→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   101→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   102→            explain=explain,
   103→        ),
   104→    )
   105→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   106→
   107→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   108→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   109→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   110→        state,
   111→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   115→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative
   116→    )
   117→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   118→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   119→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   120→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   121→        state,
   122→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   123→    )
   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   125→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   126→    ]
   127→    write_query(payload)
   128→
   129→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   130→    if output_file:
   131→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   132→            output_file,
   133→            payload,
   134→            len(items),
   135→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   136→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   137→        ):
   138→            return
   139→        raise SystemExit(1)
   140→
   141→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   145→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   150→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   151→        state.get("findings", {}),
   152→        state.get("scan_path"),
   153→    )
   154→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   155→    try:
   156→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   157→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   158→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   159→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   160→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   161→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   162→        potentials=potentials,
   163→    )
   164→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   165→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores,
   168→        findings_scoped,
   169→        strict_score=strict_score,
   170→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   171→    )
   172→    print()
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   176→

    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→
    40→
    41→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    42→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    43→
    44→    items: list[dict]
    45→    total: int
    46→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    47→    requested_tier: int | None
    48→    selected_tier: int | None
    49→    fallback_reason: str | None
    50→    available_tiers: list[int]
    51→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    52→
    53→
    54→def build_work_queue(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    58→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    59→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    60→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    61→
    62→    status = resolved_options.status
    63→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    64→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    65→    try:
    66→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    67→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    68→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    69→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    70→
    71→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    72→        state,
    73→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    74→        status_filter=status,
    75→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    76→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    77→    )
    78→
    79→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    80→    if (
    81→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    82→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    83→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    84→    ):
    85→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    86→            state,
    87→            state.get("findings", {}),
    88→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
    89→        )
    90→        for item in subjective_items:
    91→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
    92→                all_items.append(item)
    93→
    94→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    95→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
    96→
    97→    requested_tier = (
    98→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
    99→    )
   100→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   101→    fallback_reason = None
   102→    filtered = all_items
   103→
   104→    if requested_tier is not None:
   105→        filtered = [
   106→            item
   107→            for item in all_items
   108→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   109→        ]
   110→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   111→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   112→            if chosen is not None:
   113→                selected_tier = chosen
   114→                filtered = [
   115→                    item
   116→                    for item in all_items
   117→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   118→                ]
   119→                fallback_reason = (
   120→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   121→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   122→                )
   123→        elif not filtered:
   124→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   125→
   126→    total = len(filtered)
   127→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   128→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   129→
   130→    if resolved_options.explain:
   131→        for item in filtered:
   132→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   133→
   134→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   135→    return {
   136→        "items": filtered,
   137→        "total": total,
   138→        "tier_counts": counts,
   139→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   140→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   141→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   142→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   144→    }
   145→

   152→    "group_queue_items",
   153→]
