```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        else:
   182→            key = "items"
   183→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   184→    return grouped
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = [
   188→    "build_finding_items",
   189→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   190→    "item_explain",
   191→    "item_sort_key",
   192→    "tier_counts",
   193→    "subjective_score_value",
   194→    "group_queue_items",
   195→]
   196→
```

```
    39→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    40→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    41→    try:
    42→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    43→    except OSError as exc:
    44→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    45→        sys.exit(1)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    49→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    50→    try:
    51→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    52→    except OSError as exc:
    53→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    54→        sys.exit(1)
    55→
    56→
    57→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    59→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    60→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    61→
    62→    state_file = state_path(args)
    63→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    64→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    65→        state,
    66→        args,
    67→        attestation=attestation,
    68→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    69→    )
    70→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    71→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    72→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    73→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    74→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    78→    if not all_resolved:
    79→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    89→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
    90→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    91→        state,
    92→        status=args.status,
    93→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
    94→    )
    95→    has_review_findings = any(
    96→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
    97→        for fid in all_resolved
    98→    )
    99→    _print_score_movement(
   100→        status=args.status,
   101→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   102→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   103→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   104→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   105→        state=state,
   106→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   107→    )
   108→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   109→        args=args,
   110→        state=state,
   111→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   112→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   116→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   117→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   118→        state,
   119→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   120→    )
   121→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   122→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   123→
   124→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   125→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   126→        ResolveQueryContext(
   127→            patterns=args.patterns,
   128→            status=args.status,
   129→            resolved=all_resolved,
   130→            next_command=next_command,
   131→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   132→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   133→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   134→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   135→            attestation=attestation,
   136→            narrative=narrative,
   137→            state=state,
   138→        )
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   143→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   144→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   145→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
   146→        _show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   147→        sys.exit(1)
   148→
   149→    state_file = state_path(args)
   150→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   151→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   152→
   153→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   154→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   155→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   156→
   157→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   158→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   159→        {
   160→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   161→            "command": "ignore",
   162→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   163→            "attestation": attestation,
   164→            "affected": removed,
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   168→
   169→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   170→    if removed:
   171→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   172→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   173→
   174→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   175→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   176→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   177→        state,
   178→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   179→    )
   180→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   181→    write_query(

   189→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   190→            "attestation": attestation,
   191→            "narrative": narrative,
   192→        }
   193→    )
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→
```

```
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
```

```
1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import TIER_LABELS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    15→
    16→
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState) -> str:
   292→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
   293→    findings = state["findings"]
   294→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   295→
   296→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   297→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   298→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   299→    lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   300→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   301→
   302→    return "\n".join(lines)
   303→
```

```
1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    """
    23→    result = build_work_queue(
    24→        state,
    25→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    26→            tier=tier,
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→            no_tier_fallback=True,
    32→        ),
    33→    )
    34→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_next_item(
    38→    state: PlanState,
    39→    tier: int | None = None,
    40→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    41→) -> PlanItem | None:
    42→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    43→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    44→    return items[0] if items else None
    45→
```

```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def _validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str,
    51→    attestation: str | None,
    52→    example: str,
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        _show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   101→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   105→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   106→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for pattern in patterns:
   108→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   109→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   110→            if finding_id:
   111→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   112→    return len(matched_ids)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> float:
   122→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   123→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   124→    if before is None:
   125→        return 0.0
   126→
   127→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   128→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   130→    if after is None:
   131→        return 0.0
   132→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    *,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   146→    if preview_count <= 10:
   147→        return
   148→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   149→        return
   150→
```

```
    29→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    30→workflow:
    31→  scan                          Run all detectors, update state, show diff
    32→  status                        Score dashboard with per-tier progress
    33→  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    34→  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
    35→  resolve <pattern> <status>    Mark findings as open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive
    36→  ignore <pattern>              Suppress findings matching a pattern
    37→  zone show                     Show zone classifications for all files
    38→  zone set <file> <zone>        Override zone for a file
    39→  review --prepare              Prepare holistic codebase review data
    40→  review --import FILE          Import review findings from JSON
    41→  review --validate-import FILE Validate import payload/trust mode without mutating state
    42→  review --external-start       Start external blind review session (Claude cloud)
    43→  review --external-submit      Submit external session results with canonical provenance
    44→  review --merge                Merge duplicate review findings
    45→  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan
    46→
    47→examples:
    48→  desloppify scan --skip-slow
    49→  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
    50→  desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
    51→  desloppify tree --detail --focus shared/components/MediaLightbox --min-loc 300
    52→  desloppify show src/shared/components/PromptEditorModal.tsx
    53→  desloppify show gods
    54→  desloppify show "src/shared/components/MediaLightbox"
    55→  desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --note "removed unused React import" --attest "I have actually removed the unused React import from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    56→  desloppify resolve fixed "logs::src/foo.tsx::*" --note "removed debug logs" --attest "I have actually removed all console.log calls from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    57→  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated --note "migration in progress" --attest "I have actually verified these are tracked in the migration plan and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    58→  desloppify ignore "smells::*::async_no_await" --attest "I have actually verified these are intentional fire-and-forget patterns and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    59→  desloppify detect logs --top 10
    60→  desloppify detect dupes --threshold 0.9
    61→  desloppify review --validate-import findings.json --attested-external --attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness of overall score and is unbiased."
    62→  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
    63→  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <session_id> --import findings.json
    64→  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --retrospective
    65→  desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod --default-src .
    66→  desloppify move src/shared/hooks/useFoo.ts src/shared/hooks/video/useFoo.ts --dry-run
    67→  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
    68→"""
    69→
    70→
    71→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    72→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    73→
    74→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    75→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    76→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    80→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    81→    try:
    82→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    83→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    84→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    85→
    86→
    87→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    88→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    89→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    90→
    91→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    92→        prog="desloppify",
    93→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    94→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    95→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    96→    )
    97→    parser.add_argument(
    98→        "--lang",
    99→        type=str,
   100→        default=None,
   101→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   102→    )
   103→    parser.add_argument(
   104→        "--exclude",
   105→        action="append",
   106→        default=None,
   107→        metavar="PATTERN",
   108→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   109→    )
   110→    parser.add_argument(
   111→        "--version",
   112→        "-V",
   113→        action="version",
   114→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   115→    )
   116→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   117→        dest="command",
   118→        required=True,
   119→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   120→    )
   121→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_resolve_parser(sub)
   127→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   129→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   130→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   132→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   137→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   138→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   139→    return parser
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
```

```
1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue
    81→        if not detail_dict(finding).get("holistic"):
    82→            continue
    83→
    84→        last_seen = finding.get("last_seen")
    85→        if not last_seen:
    86→            continue
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            seen_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_seen)
    90→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    91→            logger.debug(
    92→                "Skipping holistic finding %s with invalid last_seen %r: %s",
    93→                finding_id,
    94→                last_seen,
    95→                exc,
    96→            )
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        age_days = (now - seen_dt).days
   100→        if age_days > max_age_days:
   101→            finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   102→            finding["resolved_at"] = now.isoformat()
   103→            finding["note"] = "holistic review expired — re-run review to re-evaluate"
   104→            expired.append(finding_id)
   105→
   106→    return expired
   107→
```

```
    41→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    42→    p_scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff")
    43→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    44→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
    45→    p_scan.add_argument(
    46→        "--reset-subjective",
    47→        action="store_true",
    48→        help="Reset subjective measures to 0 before running scan",
    49→    )
    50→    p_scan.add_argument(
    51→        "--skip-slow", action="store_true", help="Skip slow detectors (dupes)"
    52→    )
    53→    p_scan.add_argument(
    54→        "--profile",
    55→        choices=["objective", "full", "ci"],
    56→        default=None,
    57→        help="Scan profile: objective, full, or ci",
    58→    )
    59→    p_scan.add_argument(
    60→        "--force-resolve",
    61→        action="store_true",
```

```
1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    13→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    14→    state_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
    15→    if not state_dir.exists():
    16→        return None
    17→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    18→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    19→        return candidates[0]
    20→    return None
    21→
    22→
    23→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args) -> bool:
    24→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    25→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    26→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    27→
    28→
    29→def state_path(args) -> Path | None:
    30→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    31→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    32→    if path_arg:
    33→        return Path(path_arg)
    34→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    35→    if not lang_name:
    36→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    37→    if lang_name:
    38→        resolved = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    39→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    40→            return resolved
    41→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    42→        if fallback is not None:
    43→            return fallback
    44→        return resolved
    45→
    46→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    47→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    48→        if fallback is not None:
    49→            return fallback
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    55→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    56→    if not has_completed_scan:
    57→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    58→    return has_completed_scan
    59→
```

```
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)

   389→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   390→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   391→    for lang_name in langs:
   392→        try:
   393→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   394→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   395→            fixer_names = []
   396→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   397→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   398→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   399→    return fixer_help_lines
```

```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→
    17→
    18→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    19→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    20→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ResolveQueryContext:
    25→    patterns: list[str]
    26→    status: str
    27→    resolved: list[str]
    28→    next_command: str
    29→    prev_overall: float | None
    30→    prev_objective: float | None
    31→    prev_strict: float | None
    32→    prev_verified: float | None
    33→    attestation: str | None
    34→    narrative: dict
    35→    state: dict
    36→
    37→
    38→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    40→    return [
    41→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    42→    ]
    43→
    44→
    45→def _validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    46→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _show_attestation_requirement(
    50→    label: str,
    51→    attestation: str | None,
    52→    example: str,
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        _show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
```

```
    19→def _validate_runner(runner: str, *, colorize_fn) -> None:

    33→    packet: dict,
    34→    *,
    35→    colorize_fn,
    36→    suggested_prepare_cmd: str | None = None,
    37→) -> list[dict]:

    57→            "dim",

    64→def _print_review_quality(quality: object, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    65→    """Render merged review quality summary when present."""
    66→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
    67→        return
    68→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
    69→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
    70→    high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
    71→        REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    72→    )
    73→    if not isinstance(high_missing_issue_note, int | float):
    74→        high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
    75→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    76→        )
    77→    finding_pressure = quality.get("finding_pressure")
    78→    dims_with_findings = quality.get("dimensions_with_findings")
    79→    if not isinstance(coverage, int | float) or not isinstance(density, int | float):
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    pressure_segment = ""
    83→    if isinstance(finding_pressure, int | float) and isinstance(dims_with_findings, int):
    84→        pressure_segment = (
    85→            f", finding-pressure {float(finding_pressure):.2f} "
    86→            f"across {dims_with_findings} dims"
    87→        )
    88→    print(
    89→        colorize_fn(
    90→            "  Review quality: "
    91→            f"dimension coverage {float(coverage):.2f}, "
    92→            f"evidence density {float(density):.2f}, "
    93→            f"high-score-missing-issue-note {int(high_missing_issue_note or 0)}"
    94→            f"{pressure_segment}",
    95→            "dim",
    96→        )
    97→    )
```
