```
    99→
   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed
   128→
   129→
   130→def _coerce_positive_int(value: object, *, default: int, minimum: int = 1) -> int:
   131→    """Parse positive integer CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   132→    if value is None:
   133→        return default
   134→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   135→        return default
   136→    try:
   137→        parsed = int(value)
   138→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   139→        return default
   140→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   141→
   142→
   143→def _coerce_positive_float(value: object, *, default: float, minimum: float = 0.1) -> float:
   144→    """Parse positive float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   145→    if value is None:
   146→        return default
   147→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   148→        return default
   149→    try:
   150→        parsed = float(value)
   151→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   152→        return default
   153→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   154→
   155→
   156→def _coerce_non_negative_float(value: object, *, default: float) -> float:
   157→    """Parse non-negative float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   158→    if value is None:
   159→        return default
   160→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   161→        return default
   162→    try:
   163→        parsed = float(value)
   164→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165→        return default
   166→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default
   167→
   168→
   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   172→        return []
   173→    out: list[str] = []
   174→    seen: set[str] = set()
   175→    for item in raw:
   176→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   177→            continue
   178→        dim = item.strip()
   179→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   180→            continue
   181→        seen.add(dim)
   182→        out.append(dim)
   183→    return out
   184→
   185→
   186→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   187→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   188→    try:
   189→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   190→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   191→        return []
   192→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
   193→
   194→
   195→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   196→    *,
   197→    selected_dims: list[str],
   198→    scored_dims: list[str],
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    selected = set(selected_dims)
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
    38→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):
    39→    """Raised when review import CLI flags are mutually incompatible."""
    40→
    41→
    42→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    43→    *,
    44→    attested_external: bool,
    45→    allow_partial: bool,
    46→    override_enabled: bool,
    47→    override_attest: str | None,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    50→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    51→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    52→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    53→        )
    54→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    55→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    56→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    57→        )
    58→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
    59→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    60→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    61→        )
    62→    if override_enabled and (
    63→        not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip()
    64→    ):
    65→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    69→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    70→    raw_assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
    71→    keys: set[str] = set()
    72→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    73→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    74→        if normalized:
    75→            keys.add(normalized)
    76→    return keys
    77→
    78→
    79→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    80→    state: dict,
    81→    *,
    82→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    83→) -> int:
    84→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    85→    if not assessment_keys:
    86→        return 0
    87→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    88→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    89→        return 0
    90→
    91→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
    92→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
    93→    marked = 0
    94→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
    95→        payload = store.get(key)
    96→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    97→            continue
    98→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
    99→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   100→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   101→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   102→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   103→        marked += 1
   104→    return marked
   105→
   106→
   107→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   108→    state: dict,
   109→    *,
   110→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   111→) -> int:
   112→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   113→    if not assessment_keys:
   114→        return 0
   115→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   116→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   117→        return 0
   118→
   119→    cleared = 0
   120→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   121→        payload = store.get(key)
   122→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   123→            continue
   124→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   125→            cleared += 1
   126→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   127→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   128→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   129→    return cleared
   130→
   131→
   132→def do_import(
   133→    import_file,
   134→    state,
   135→    lang,
   136→    state_file,
   137→    *,
   138→    config: dict | None = None,
   139→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   140→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   141→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   142→    attested_external: bool = False,
   143→    manual_override: bool = False,
   144→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   145→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   146→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   147→) -> None:
   148→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced findings."""
   149→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   150→        manual_override=manual_override,
   151→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   152→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   153→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   154→    )
   155→    try:
   156→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   157→            attested_external=attested_external,
   158→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   159→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   160→            override_attest=override_attest,
   161→        )
   162→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   163→        print(colorize(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   164→        sys.exit(1)
   165→
   166→    try:
   167→        findings_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_findings_data(
   168→            import_file,
   169→            lang_name=lang.name,
   170→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   171→            trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   172→            trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   173→            attested_external=attested_external,
   174→            manual_override=override_enabled,
   175→            manual_attest=override_attest,
   176→            assessment_override=assessment_override,
   177→            assessment_note=assessment_note,
   178→        )
   179→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   180→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   181→            exc.errors,
   182→            import_file=str(import_file),
   183→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   184→        )
   185→        sys.exit(1)
   186→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   187→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(findings_data)
   188→    )
   189→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   190→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   191→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   192→    )
   193→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   194→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   195→        import_file=str(import_file),
   196→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   197→    )
   198→
   199→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   200→
   201→    # Transactional import: only persist if all post-import guards pass.
   202→    # Rebase on the latest on-disk state when available so long-running review
   203→    # sessions don't clobber newer imports/scans that completed while batches ran.
   204→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   205→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   206→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   207→    else:
   208→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   209→    diff = review_mod.import_holistic_findings(findings_data, working_state, lang.name)
   210→    label = "Holistic review"
   211→    imported_assessment_keys = _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data)
   212→    provisional_count = 0
   213→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   214→        provisional_count = _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   215→            working_state,
   216→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   217→        )
   218→    elif assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   219→        _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   220→            working_state,
   221→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   222→        )
   223→
   224→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) > 0 and not allow_partial:
   225→        print(
   226→            colorize(
   227→                "  Error: import produced skipped finding(s); refusing partial import.",
   228→                "red",
   229→            ),
   230→            file=sys.stderr,
   231→        )
   232→        for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   233→            reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   234→            print(
   235→                colorize(
   236→                    f"    #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}",
   237→                    "red",
   238→                ),
   239→                file=sys.stderr,
   240→            )
   241→        print(
   242→            colorize(
   243→                "  Fix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override.",
   244→                "yellow",
   245→            ),
   246→            file=sys.stderr,
   247→        )
   248→        sys.exit(1)
   249→
   250→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   251→        audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   252→        audit.append(
   253→            {
   254→                "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   255→                "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   256→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   257→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   258→                "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   259→                "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   260→                "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   261→                "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   262→                "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   263→                "import_file": str(import_file),
   264→            }
   265→        )
   266→    state.clear()
   267→    state.update(working_state)
   268→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   269→
   270→    lang_name = lang.name
   271→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   272→        state, NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review")
   273→    )
   274→
   275→    print(colorize(f"\n  {label} imported:", "bold"))
   276→    issue_count = int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0)
   277→    print(
   278→        colorize(
   279→            f"  +{issue_count} new issue{'s' if issue_count != 1 else ''} "
   280→            f"(review findings), "
   281→            f"{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved, "
   282→            f"{diff['reopened']} reopened",
   283→            "dim",
   284→        )
   285→    )
   286→    if provisional_count > 0:
   287→        print(
   288→            colorize(
   289→                "  WARNING: manual override assessments are provisional and will "
   290→                "reset on the next scan unless replaced by "
   291→                "`review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   292→                "yellow",
   293→            )
   294→        )
   295→    import_helpers_mod.print_skipped_validation_details(diff, colorize_fn=colorize)
   296→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessments_summary(state, colorize_fn=colorize)
   297→    next_command = import_helpers_mod.print_open_review_summary(
   298→        state, colorize_fn=colorize
   299→    )
   300→    print_score_update(state, prev, config=config)
```

```
    33→
    34→
    35→def parse_holistic_import_payload(
    36→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
    37→) -> tuple[list[ReviewFindingPayload], dict[str, Any] | None, list[str]]:
    38→    """Parse strict holistic import payload object."""
    39→    payload = parse_review_import_payload(data, mode_name="Holistic")
    40→    return payload.findings, payload.assessments, payload.reviewed_files
    41→
    42→
    43→def update_reviewed_file_cache(
    44→    state: dict[str, Any],
    45→    reviewed_files: list[str],
    46→    *,
    47→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
    48→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    49→) -> None:
    50→    """Refresh per-file review cache entries from holistic payload metadata."""
    51→    refresh_review_file_cache(
    52→        state,
    53→        reviewed_files=reviewed_files,
    54→        findings_by_file=None,
    55→        project_root=project_root,
    56→        hash_file_fn=hash_file,
    57→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→_POSITIVE_PREFIXES = (
    62→    "good ",
    63→    "well ",
    64→    "strong ",
    65→    "clean ",
    66→    "excellent ",
    67→    "nice ",
    68→    "solid ",
    69→)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _validate_and_build_findings(
    73→    findings_list: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
    74→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    75→    lang_name: str,
    76→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    77→    """Validate raw holistic findings and build state-ready finding dicts.
    78→
    79→    Returns (review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    80→    """
    81→    review_findings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    82→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    83→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    84→    allowed_dimensions = {
    85→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    for idx, raw_finding in enumerate(findings_list):
    89→        finding, finding_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
    90→            raw_finding,
    91→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
    92→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    93→            allow_dismissed=True,
    94→        )
    95→        if finding_errors:
    96→            skipped.append(
    97→                {
    98→                    "index": idx,
    99→                    "missing": finding_errors,
   100→                    "identifier": (
   101→                        raw_finding.get("identifier", "<none>")
   102→                        if isinstance(raw_finding, dict)
   103→                        else "<none>"
   104→                    ),
   105→                }
   106→            )
   107→            continue
   108→        assert finding is not None
   109→
   110→        # Handle dismissed concern verdicts (no dimension/summary required).
   111→        if finding.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   112→            fp = finding.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   113→            if fp:
   114→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   115→                    {
   116→                        "fingerprint": fp,
   117→                        "concern_type": finding.get("concern_type", ""),
   118→                        "concern_file": finding.get("concern_file", ""),
   119→                        "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   120→                    }
   121→                )
   122→            continue
   123→
   124→        # Safety net: skip positive observations that slipped past the prompt.
   125→        summary_text = str(finding.get("summary", ""))
   126→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   127→            skipped.append(
   128→                {
   129→                    "index": idx,
   130→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   131→                    "identifier": finding.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   132→                }
   133→            )
   134→            continue
   135→
   136→        dimension = finding["dimension"]
   137→
   138→        # Confirmed concern verdicts become "concerns" detector findings.
   139→        is_confirmed_concern = finding.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   140→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   141→
   142→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   143→        detail: dict[str, Any] = {
   144→            "holistic": True,
   145→            "dimension": dimension,
   146→            "related_files": finding["related_files"],
   147→            "evidence": finding["evidence"],
   148→            "suggestion": finding.get("suggestion", ""),
   149→            "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   150→        }
   151→        if is_confirmed_concern:
   152→            detail["concern_type"] = finding.get("concern_type", "")
   153→            detail["concern_verdict"] = "confirmed"
   154→
   155→        prefix = "concern" if is_confirmed_concern else "holistic"
   156→        file = finding.get("concern_file", "") if is_confirmed_concern else ""
   157→        confidence = normalize_review_confidence(finding.get("confidence", "low"))
   158→        imported = make_finding(
   159→            detector=detector,
   160→            file=file,
   161→            name=f"{prefix}::{dimension}::{finding['identifier']}::{content_hash}",
   162→            tier=review_tier(confidence, holistic=True),
   163→            confidence=confidence,
   164→            summary=summary_text,
   165→            detail=detail,
   166→        )
   167→        imported["lang"] = lang_name
   168→        review_findings.append(imported)
   169→
   170→    return review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns
   171→
   172→
   173→def _collect_imported_dimensions(
   174→    *,
   175→    findings_list: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
   176→    review_findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   177→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None,
   178→    review_scope: ReviewScopePayload | dict[str, Any] | None,
   179→    valid_dimensions: set[str],
   180→) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return normalized dimensions this import explicitly covered."""
   182→    imported_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   183→
   184→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   185→        scope_dims = review_scope.get("imported_dimensions")
   186→        if isinstance(scope_dims, list):
   187→            for raw_dim in scope_dims:
   188→                normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   189→                if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   190→                    imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   191→
   192→    for finding in findings_list:
   193→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   194→            continue
   195→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(finding.get("dimension", "")))
   196→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   197→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   198→
   199→    for finding in review_findings:
   200→        detail = finding.get("detail")
   201→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   202→            continue
   203→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   204→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   205→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   206→
   207→    for raw_dim in (assessments or {}):
   208→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   209→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   210→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   211→
   212→    return imported_dimensions
   213→
   214→
   215→def _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   216→    state: dict[str, Any],
   217→    new_ids: set[str],
   218→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   219→    utc_now_fn,
   220→    *,
   221→    imported_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   222→    full_sweep_included: bool | None = None,
   223→) -> None:
   224→    """Auto-resolve open holistic findings not present in the latest import."""
   225→    scope_dimensions = {
   226→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   227→        for dim in (imported_dimensions or set())
   228→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   229→    }
   230→    scoped_reimport = full_sweep_included is False
   231→    # Partial re-import with unknown dimension scope: do not auto-resolve.
   232→    if scoped_reimport and not scope_dimensions:
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    def _should_resolve(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   236→        if finding.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns"):
   237→            return False
   238→        detail = finding.get("detail")
   239→        if not isinstance(detail, dict) or not detail.get("holistic"):
   240→            return False
   241→        if not scoped_reimport:
   242→            return True
   243→        dimension = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   244→        return dimension in scope_dimensions
   245→
   246→    auto_resolve_review_findings(
   247→        state,
   248→        new_ids=new_ids,
   249→        diff=diff,
   250→        note="not reported in latest holistic re-import",
```

```
    15→def is_subjective_phase(phase) -> bool:
    16→    """Return True when a phase is subjective/review oriented."""
    17→    label = (phase.label or "").lower()
    18→    run_name = getattr(phase.run, "__name__", "").lower()
    19→    return (

    22→        or run_name == "phase_subjective_review"
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     6→    QueueBuildOptions,

    11→
    12→
    13→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    14→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    15→
    16→
    17→def _finding(
    18→    fid: str,
    19→    *,
    20→    detector: str = "smells",
    21→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    22→    tier: int = 3,
    23→    confidence: str = "medium",
    24→    status: str = "open",
    25→    detail: dict | None = None,
    26→) -> dict:
    27→    return {
    28→        "id": fid,
    29→        "detector": detector,
    30→        "file": file,
    31→        "tier": tier,
    32→        "confidence": confidence,
    33→        "summary": fid,
    34→        "status": status,
    35→        "detail": detail or {},
    36→    }
    37→
    38→
    39→def _state(findings: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    40→    return {
    41→        "findings": {f["id"]: f for f in findings},
    42→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    43→    }
    44→
    45→
    46→def test_tier_fallback_selects_nearest_non_empty_tier():
    47→    state = _state(
    48→        [
    49→            _finding("t2_item", tier=2),
    50→            _finding("t4_item", tier=4),
    51→        ]
    52→    )
    53→
    54→    queue = build_work_queue(state, tier=1, count=None)
    55→    assert queue["requested_tier"] == 1
    56→    assert queue["selected_tier"] == 2
    57→    assert (
    58→        queue["fallback_reason"]
    59→        == "Requested T1 has 0 open -> showing T2 (nearest non-empty)."
    60→    )
    61→    assert [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]] == ["t2_item"]
    62→
    63→
    64→def test_no_tier_fallback_returns_empty_with_reason():
    65→    state = _state([_finding("t2_item", tier=2)])
    66→
    67→    queue = build_work_queue(state, tier=4, count=None, no_tier_fallback=True)
    68→    assert queue["requested_tier"] == 4
    69→    assert queue["selected_tier"] == 4
    70→    assert queue["items"] == []
    71→    assert queue["fallback_reason"] == "Requested T4 has 0 open."
    72→
    73→
    74→def test_review_finding_uses_natural_tier():

    76→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    77→        detector="review",
    78→        tier=2,
    79→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
```

```
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:

    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)

    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None

    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):

    89→            "gap": None,

    91→            "warning": warning,

    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:

    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
```

## Comprehensive Review Command Architecture Analysis

Based on my thorough code exploration, here are the answers to your five key questions:

---

### **1. Review Command Entry Points**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/entrypoint.py`

The review command is dispatched through `cmd_review()` which handles five distinct modes:

- **`--prepare`** (default): Calls `do_prepare()` — generates holistic review packet for `query.json`
- **`--run-batches`**: Calls `_do_run_batches()` (in `batch.py`) — executes subagent batches and imports results
- **`--external-start`**: Calls `do_external_start()` (in `external.py`) — initiates blind cloud review session
- **`--external-submit`**: Calls `do_external_submit()` (in `external.py`) — submits external results with canonical provenance
- **`--import <file>`**: Calls `do_import()` (in `import_cmd.py`) — imports review findings from JSON
- **`--validate-import <file>`**: Calls `do_validate_import()` — validates without state mutation

All modes first resolve the language via `resolve_lang(args)` and load state/config via `command_runtime()`.

---

### **2. Plan/Queue System**

**Core queue system files:**
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`** — Main `build_work_queue()` orchestrator
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`** — Item construction: `build_subjective_items()`, `build_finding_items()`

**How it tracks "open actionable plan items":**

1. **Finding items** (objective): Built from `state["findings"]` filtered by status="open"
2. **Subjective items** (tier 4): Built synthetically from `scorecard_subjective_entries()` when:
   - Dimension score is below threshold (default: 100%)
   - Dimension exists in `state["dimension_scores"]`
   - Not all dimensions are assessed yet OR dimension is marked `stale`

**Stale subjective tracking in work queue:**
```python
# engine/_work_queue/helpers.py:250
is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
# ...
stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
```

The work queue reads the `"stale"` flag from `scorecard_subjective_entries()` which derives it from:
```python
# engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py:103-107
stale = bool(
    dim_key
    and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
    and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
)
```

**Plan command** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan_cmd.py`):
- Calls `planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)` which uses `build_work_queue()` internally
- Returns markdown plan with tier-organized task list

### **3. Stale Subjective Entries: Creation, Tracking, Clearing**

**Where `needs_review_refresh` is SET:**

1. **On mechanical finding changes** (`engine/_state/merge.py:87`):
   ```python
   # When structural/smells/naming/etc. detectors change
   payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
   payload["stale_since"] = utc_now()
   ```
   Maps: `_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS` (e.g., `"structural"` → `("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness")`)

2. **On review finding resolution** (`engine/_state/resolution.py:71-73`):
   ```python
   # When a review finding is resolved (fixed/wontfix/false_positive)
   payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
   payload["stale_since"] = now
   ```

**How stale entries are CLEARED:**

Currently, there is **no explicit "clearing" operation**. Instead, clearing happens implicitly during:

1. **Assessment import** (`app/commands/review/import_cmd.py:219-222`):
   ```python
   elif assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
       _clear_provisional_override_flags(
           working_state,
           assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
       )
   ```
   This clears `provisional_override` but does NOT clear `needs_review_refresh`

2. **On fresh import through `store_assessments()`** (`intelligence/review/importing/shared.py`):
   The imported assessment payload likely overwrites the entire entry, replacing `needs_review_refresh: True` with new score + metadata

**Key insight:** The stale flag persists in state until an import replaces the entire assessment entry with fresh data.

---

### **4. Existing Preflight/Guard Patterns**

**Model pattern: `_validate_resolve_inputs()`** 
Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/selection.py:80-96`

```python
def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
        _emit_warning("Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note...")
        sys.exit(1)
    if args.status == "open":
        return
    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
        _show_attestation_requirement(...)
        sys.exit(2)
```

**Pattern elements:**
- Runs **before** the main operation
- Emits warnings to stderr with `colorize(message, "yellow")`
- Exits with specific codes (1 = precondition failed, 2 = validation error)
- Checks multiple preconditions in sequence
- Can accept contextual data (patterns, status) for better error messages

**Other guards in resolve flow:**
- `_enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation()` — Requires `--confirm-batch-wontfix` flag for >10 items
- `_previous_score_snapshot()` — Snapshots scores before operations for comparison
- `_validate_attestation()` — Ensures required keywords ("i have actually", "not gaming") are present

**For review import:**
- `_validate_import_flag_combos()` (`app/commands/review/import_cmd.py:42-65`) — Ensures flag combinations are valid
- Payload validation happens in `import_helpers_mod.load_import_findings_data()`

   ```python
       args: argparse.Namespace,
   ```

## Summary: Plan Items and Work Queue Management in Desloppify

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here's the complete picture of how plan items and actionable work queue items are managed:

### 1. **Plan Command Implementation**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan_cmd.py`

```python
def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    state = command_runtime(args).state
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return
    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
```

**What it contains:**
- Generated via `planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)` 
- Includes:
  - Header with scores (overall, objective, strict) and stats
  - Dimension health table (all dimensions with tier, checks, issues, health %)
  - Per-tier sections (T1-T4) with file grouping
  - Addressed section (fixed/wontfix/auto_resolved findings)

**Data structure:** String (Markdown), not typed plan items—it's rendered from state findings.

---

### 2. **Work Queue Architecture**
**Public API:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/work_queue.py`  
**Core implementation:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

**Key types:**

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    tier: int | None = None
    count: int | None = 1
    scan_path: str | None = None
    scope: str | None = None
    status: str = "open"  # Valid: "open", "all", "fixed", "wontfix", etc.
    include_subjective: bool = True
    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    chronic: bool = False
    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    explain: bool = False

class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    items: list[dict]           # Ranked queue items
    total: int                   # Total count of items in queue
    tier_counts: dict[int, int]  # Count per tier {1: N, 2: N, 3: N, 4: N}
    requested_tier: int | None
    selected_tier: int | None
    fallback_reason: str | None
    available_tiers: list[int]   # Tiers with > 0 items
    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]  # Items grouped by file/detector/tier
```

**Building a queue:**
```python
from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_work_queue, QueueBuildOptions

queue = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        tier=None,           # Request specific tier, or None for all
        count=20,            # Limit to N items
        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
        status="open",       # Filter by status
        include_subjective=True,  # Include synthetic subjective items
        subjective_threshold=95.0  # Only subjective items below this score
    ),
)

items = queue.get("items", [])      # Ranked work items
total = queue.get("total", 0)       # Total count
```

---

### 3. **Item Types: Objective vs Subjective**

**Objective findings** (`kind="finding"`):
- Real detector findings (smells, unused vars, dupes, review, etc.)
- Built via `build_finding_items()` from `state["findings"]`
- Each finding has: `id`, `detector`, `file`, `tier`, `confidence`, `summary`, `status`
- Status-filtered: "open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"

**Subjective findings** (`kind="subjective_dimension"`):
- Synthetic items built by `build_subjective_items()` 
- Always tier 4, created when dimension score < subjective_threshold
- Have `id=f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}"`, `detector="subjective_assessment"`
- Include: dimension name, strict_score, open_review_findings count, cli_keys

**Built together:**
```python
# In core.py:build_work_queue()
finding_items = build_finding_items(state, ...)  # Objective findings
all_items = list(finding_items)

if options.include_subjective and status in {"open", "all"}:
    subjective_items = build_subjective_items(state, ...)
    all_items.extend(subjective_items)

all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)  # Ranked by tier, confidence, weight
```

---

### 4. **Ranking/Sorting Logic**

**Sort key for objective findings:**
```python
def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    if item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (
            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
            1,  # Subjective sorts after mechanical within T4
            subjective_score_value(item),
            item.get("id", ""),
        )
    return (
        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
        0,
        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence"), 9),  # high=0, medium=1, low=2
        -review_finding_weight(item),
        -int(detail.get("count", 0)),
        item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

**Tier labels:**
```python
TIER_LABELS = {
    1: "Highest priority (auto-fixable + high-confidence holistic review)",
    2: "Quick fixes (unused vars, dead exports, exact dupes, orphaned files)",
    3: "Needs judgment (smells, near-dupes, single-use, cycles, state sync)",
    4: "Major refactors (structural decomposition, large import cycles)",
}
```

---

### 5. **Next Command Implementation**

```python
def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return
    
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            tier=getattr(args, "tier", None),
            count=getattr(args, "count", 1),
            scope=getattr(args, "scope", None),
            status="open",
            include_subjective=True,
            subjective_threshold=95.0,
        ),
    )
    items = queue.get("items", [])
    # Render items + tier fallback info
```

### 6. **How to Count Remaining Actionable Items**

**To check if there are open actionable plan items:**

```python
from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_work_queue, QueueBuildOptions

def count_open_actionable_items(state: dict) -> int:
    """Count all open findings + subjective items below target."""
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            include_subjective=True,
            subjective_threshold=95.0,  # Or get from config
            no_tier_fallback=True,
        ),
    )
    return queue["total"]

def has_actionable_items(state: dict) -> bool:
    """Check if there are any open actionable items."""
    return count_open_actionable_items(state) > 0
```

**To check tier-specific counts:**
```python
queue = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(status="open"))
tier_counts = queue["tier_counts"]  # {1: N, 2: N, 3: N, 4: N}
available_tiers = queue["available_tiers"]  # [1, 3, 4] if those have items
```

### 7. **Guard/Precondition Patterns**

**Existing pattern in the codebase:**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/state.py`
```python
def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    if not has_completed_scan:
        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    return has_completed_scan
```

**Used in commands:**
```python
# In plan_cmd.py, next.py, show/cmd.py, etc.
if not require_completed_scan(state):
    return
```

**Resolve command validation pattern:**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/selection.py`
```python
def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
        _emit_warning("Wontfix items become technical debt...")
        sys.exit(1)
    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
        _show_attestation_requirement(...)
        sys.exit(1)
```

### 8. **Recommended Implementation for a Precondition Check**

To add a guard checking for "open actionable plan items," follow this pattern:

```python
# In desloppify/app/commands/helpers/state.py

def require_open_actionable_items(state: dict) -> bool:
    """Return True if there are open actionable items; print guidance otherwise."""
    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_work_queue, QueueBuildOptions
    
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            include_subjective=True,
            subjective_threshold=state.get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95.0),
            no_tier_fallback=True,
        ),
    )
    
    has_items = queue["total"] > 0
    if not has_items:
        print(colorize("No open actionable items remaining.", "green"))
        print(colorize(
            "Consider: desloppify next --status all (to view addressed), "
            "or run 'desloppify plan' to review progress.",
            "dim"
        ))
    return has_items

# Then in a command:
if not require_open_actionable_items(state):
    return  # or sys.exit(0) if appropriate
```

---

### 9. **Data Flow Summary**

```
State (findings dict) 
  ↓
build_finding_items() → objective findings
build_subjective_items() → subjective dimensions below threshold
  ↓
Merge + sort by item_sort_key (tier, then confidence/subjective_score)
  ↓
WorkQueueResult {items, total, tier_counts, available_tiers, grouped}
  ↓
Used by: next command, plan rendering, show command
```

All findings are stored in `state["findings"]` as a dict keyed by finding ID, with each finding having `status` ("open" | "fixed" | "wontfix" | "auto_resolved" | "false_positive"), making it easy to filter actionable items with `status == "open"` and optional scope/tier filtering.
